本行第三届董事会第五次会议于2004年4月9日审议通过了本行《2003年年度报告》。会议应到董事16名，实际到会13名，李光林、董晓云董事委托王明权，张镇德董事委托王川行使表决权。本行7名监事列席了本次会议。

本行年度会计报表已经普华永道中天会计师事务所根据中国注册会计师独立审计准则审计，并出具了无保留意见的审计报告。

Furthermore, the Bank’s Board of Directors and its Board Directors are fully responsible, both individually and collectively, for the trueness, accuracy and completeness of this report.

On April 9th, 2004, the fifth meeting of the Bank's third Board of Directors examined and approved the Bank's Annual Report 2003. The quorum of the meeting shall be 16, and 13 Directors attended the meeting. Director Mr. Li Guanglin, Mr. Dong Xiaoyun entrusted Director Mr. Wang Mingquan, Director Mr. Zhang Zhende eatrusted Mr. Wang Chuan to exercise his voting powers. Seven Supervisors also attended the meeting as non-voting delegates.

PricewaterhouseCoopers Zhongtian Public Accountants has audited the Bank’s annual financial statements according to the independent auditing Standards of China's certified accountants and issued its audit report without reservation.

#### 董事长致辞

2003年，我国经济经受住了包括非典疫情在内的严峻考验，取得了非凡的成就。全年GDP增长9.1%，人均GDP首次突破1000美元，国家的经济实力迈上一个新的台阶。在这一年里，中国光大银行认真贯彻“三个代表”的重要思想，按照中央关于金融工作的一系列方针政策，在银监会的监督指导下，在股东单位和社会各界的大力支持下，坚持发展主题，努力防范风险，加强经营管理，全行业务继续保持稳定、健康发展态势，业务发展的速度和质量有了进一步的提高，整体实力继续增强；公司治理发生明显变化，治理结构和治理机制进一步完善，现代企业制度建设取得进展；广大干部员工在市场竞争不断加剧的形势下，锐意进取，努力拓展业务，表现出较强的市场意识和敬业精神，全行的整体形象进一步提升。

2004年是我国实现“十五”计划的关键之年，也是光大银行加快上市步伐、推进精品银行建设的重要一年。中国光大银行董事会将继续以党的十六大和十六届三中全会精神为指导，认真贯彻以人为本，全面、协调、可持续的科学发展观，以为股东创造更大价值、促进银行持续稳健发展为全部工作的出发点和落脚点，进一步增强发展意识、创新意识、效益意识、股东意识和服务意识，积极推进发展与改革的各项工作，通过不断提高资产质量、提升股本回报率，体现对股东负责、对社会负责、对自身的发展负责。

董事长王明雄

## INNOVATION

Extraordinary achievement has been made in China's national economy during year two thousand and three despite grim test by series of hardships including SARS epidemic outbreak. By year end, GDP grew by 9.1% and GDP per capita exceeded USD1,000.00 for the first time, showing that the national economic strength has escalated into a higher level. During the year, by adhering to the ideology of "three representations", following the policies made by the central government about economic and financial tasks, under the guidance and supervision by China Banking Regulatory Commission, and with great support from shareholders and people from all walks of life, China Everbright Bank (the Bank) held to the theme of development, made more efforts in risk mitigation, and improved its own business operation and management. As a result, the Bank maintained a stable and healthy development in all business sectors, achieved continuous improvement in both speed and quality of business development, and further enhanced its overall strength. Moreover, substantial changes have taken place in the aspect of corporate governance and significant progress has been made in further improving governance structure and mechanism, which brings the bank closer to the establishment of a modern company system. Faced with ever-intensified market competition, all bank employees were fully mobilized to promote business development, and showed strong market orientation and high devotion to their career. Therefore, the social image of the Bank was further enhanced.

Year two thousand and four is a crucial year for China to implement its tenth “five-year plan”, as is for the Bank to accelerate its pace for IPO and promoting its own evolution into a model bank. The Bank’s Board of Directors shall continue to follow the instructions set-forth by the sixteenth CPC national congress and the third plenary session of sixteenth CPC central committee, and faithfully carry out scientific approach in achieving all-round, balanced and sustainable development based on effective

human management. By setting maximization of shareholders' value and promotion of sustainable development as center goals for its entire efforts, the Bank shall further enhance the consensus in business development, product innovation, sound profitability, shareholder interests protection, and best services, proactively improve all aspects in business development and reform, and fulfill its commitment to shareholders, society at large as well as the Bank's own development through persistent improvement in asset quality and return on equity.

CHAIRMAN 王月雅

### 行 长 致 辞

2003 年，中国光大银行以党的十六大和十六届三中全会精神为指引，克服 “非典” 影响，突出发展主题，深化改革创新，严格内部管理，广泛开展 “双整顿、双提高” 活动，加强队伍建设和行风建设，有力促进了各项业务的稳定、持续、快速发展，实现了规模、质量与效益的同步增长。截止到 2003 年末，全行资产总额达 3944 亿元，比上年增长 24%，各项贷款余额 2486 亿元，比上年增长 30%，一般存款余额 3439 亿元，比上年增长 28%，按四级分类的不良资产率和不良贷款率分别比上年下降 4.10 个百分点和 4.94 个百分点。实现经营利润 26.5 亿元，比上年增长 25%；账面税前利润 8.15 亿元，比上年增长 33%。这些成绩是在银行董事会的正确领导下，在银监会和银行监事会的有效监督下，全行广大干部员工团结协作、奋力拼搏的结果。

2004年，中国光大银行高级管理层将继续认真实践“三个代表”的重要思想，牢固树立以人为本，全面、协调、可持续的科学发展观，以提高质量和效益为核心，以调整结构和改进经营管理方式为主线，以改革创新为动力，不断加大市场营销和产品创新力度，严格控制和大力化解不良资产，全面加强资产负债综合管理，进一步增收节支，切实加强内控制度建设、队伍建设和企业文化建设，坚定信心，振奋精神，求真务实，与时俱进，大力促进各项业务稳定快速持续发展，为实现银行阶段性上市目标和建设精品银行的长远目标而努力奋斗！

行长

## MODEL BANK

<div style="text-align: center;"><img src="imgs/img_in_image_box_269_304_1095_1608.jpg" alt="Image" width="69%" /></div>


In year two thousand and three, China Everbright Bank (the Bank) faithfully followed the instructions set-forth by the sixteenth CPC national congress and the third plenary session of sixteenth CPC central committee, and successfully overcame the negative impact of SARS epidemic outbreak. By sticking to the theme of development, furthering reform and innovation, strengthening internal control and management, extensively carrying out “dual consolidation and dual improvement” program, enhancing human resources management and career professionalism, the Bank was awarded with steady, sustainable and rapid development in all business sectors and coordinated growth and improvement in asset scale, quality and profitability. By the end of year two thousand and three, the Bank’s total assets reached RMB394.4 billion yuan, up 24% over that of the previous year, total outstanding loans reached RMB248.6 billion yuan, up 30% over that of the previous year, total corporate and savings deposits reached RMB343.9 billion yuan up 28% over that of the previous year, NPA ratio and NPL ratio according to four-category classification standard were respectively brought down by 4.10 percentage points and 4.94 percentage points. Moreover, the Bank realized pre-provisioning income of RMB2.65 billion yuan, up 25% over that of the previous year, and pre-taxation profits of RMB815 million yuan, up 33% over that of the previous year, All these achievements are inseparable from the right leadership by the Board of Directors, effective supervision by China Banking Regulatory Commission and the Bank’s Supervisory Board, joint efforts and cohesive dedication made by all bank employees.

In year two thousand and four, the Bank’s senior management shall continue to implement the ideology of “three representations”, firmly stick to scientific approach in achieving all-round, balanced and

sustainable development based on effective human management, center on quality and profitability improvement, and focus on structural and managerial reform. With continued management reform and innovation, the bank shall concentrate more efforts in marketing and product innovation, exert stricter control over asset quality, try hard in non-performing assets workout, and implement all-round asset and liability management. In addition, the Bank shall adopt concrete measures to increase revenue while controlling expenses, strengthen internal control mechanism, and foster excellent faculty team and corporate culture. Through reinforcing professional confidence and inspiration, adopting pragmatic approach, and keeping pace with the times, the Bank shall try is best to maintain stable, rapid and sustainable development, and work hard to realize both the intermediate goal of IPO and long-term goal of building itself into a model bank.

PRESIDENT

2011

#### 监事会主席致辞

2003年，中国光大银行董事会按照本行章程规定认真履行了职责，进一步完善了本行公司治理架构和机制，提高了公司治理水平，对银行经营管理重大决策提出了一系列工作原则和指导意见。高级管理层坚持发展主题，注意协调发展速度、质量和效益，依法合规开展经营活动，规范运作，带领全行员工努力工作，全面完成了年初董事会确定的经营计划，取得较好的经营业绩。普华永道中天会计师事务所出具的2003年度审计报告真实、客观地反映了中国光大银行的财务和经营状况。2004年，中国光大银行监事会将积极贯彻落实监管部门的各项监管要求，认真履行监督职能，切实维护股东的权益。

#### STATEMENT BY CHAIRMAN OF THE SUPERVISORY BOARD

In year two thousand and three, the Board of Director of China Everbright Bank (the Bank) faithfully exercised its powers and duties in compliance with relevant stipulations of the Bank's Articles of Association, further improved corporate governance structure and operating mechanism, raised the overall level of corporate governance, and set forth a series of principles and guidelines on significant decisions in the Bank's operation and management. The Bank's senior management held to the theme of development, focused on coordinated development in speed, quality and profitability, and conduct compliant business operation and management. As a result, through standardized operation and effective mobilization of all faculty team, the Bank's senior management realized in full the operating plan set by the Board of Directors at the year beginning. The audit report issued by Pricewater House-Coopers Zhongtian Certified Public Accountants faithfully and objectively illustrates the actual financial status and business performance of the Bank. In year two thousand and four, the Bank's Board of Supervisors shall proactively implement various regulatory requirements, and duly exercise its supervising powers and duties so as to best protect shareholder's interests.

监事会主席

CHAIRMAN,

BOARD OF SUPERVISORS

## 银行简介

### 基本情况

中国光大银行成立于1992年8月，1997年1月完成股份制改造，成为国内第一家国有控股并有国际金融组织参股的全国性股份制商业银行。11年多来，伴随着中国经济和金融业的发展进程，光大银行不断改革创新、锐意进取，始终把自身发展与国民经济的增长紧密结合，在为社会提供优质金融服务的同时，取得了良好的经营业绩，逐步形成了与现代商业银行相适应的多元化股权结构、日益完善的公司治理与经营机制、比较先进的科技支持系统、素质较高的员工队伍、布局合理的机构网络、范围广泛的同业合作等优势。截止2003年底，中国光大银行已在全国23个省、自治区、直辖市的36个经济中心城市拥有分支机构370多家，成为对社会有一定影响的全国性股份制商业银行。

法定中文名称：中国光大银行股份有限公司（简称：中国光大银行，以下称“本行”）

##### OVERVIEW

Established in August of 1992, China Everbright Bank competed to shareholding restructuring in January 1997, thus becoming a nation-wide joint-stock commercial bank with government as the controlling shareholder and, at the same time, the first domestic bank to receive equity investment from international financial institutions. In the past eleven years or so, China Everbright Bank consistently adheres to the theme of innovation and self-motivation, closely combined self-development with national economy growth, and has achieved remarkable business results while providing quality financial services to the whole society. In this process, the Bank has gradually established series of advantages in diversified shareholding structure adapted to modern commercial banking, ever improving corporate governance and operating mechanism, relatively advanced technological supporting systems, high quality faculty team, rational distribution network layout, and extensive cooperation with other financial institutions. By the end of 2003, China Everbright Bank had established over 370 banking offices in 36 major cities all over 23 provinces, autonomous regions and city provinces, and has become a nation-wide joint-stock commercial bank with modest influence on China's economic and social development.

### 主要经营指标与财务信息

## SENIOR MANAGEMENT REPORT

<div style="text-align: center;"><img src="imgs/img_in_image_box_161_607_1190_929.jpg" alt="Image" width="86%" /></div>


2003年，中国光大银行实现了规模、质量与效益的全面、协调发展，完成和超额完成了年初董事会确定的各项经营管理目标和任务。截止到2003年末，全行资产总额达到3944亿元，比年初增加752亿元，增长23.6%；各项贷款余额2486亿元，比年初增加573亿元，增长30%；其中个人贷款余额204亿元，比上年增加115亿元，增长130%。一般存款余额3439亿元，比年初增加757亿元，增长28.2%；其中储蓄存款余额375亿元，比年初增加115亿元，增长44.2%，占一般存款的比重由年初的9.7%上升到10.9%。阳光卡累计发卡量达到1267万张，比年初增加264万张，增长26.4%；卡均存款1097元，比上年增加397元。完成国际结算量200亿美元，比上年增加50亿美元，增长33%；实现国际结算非利息净收入1.92亿元，比上年增长22.8%，保理业务突破100亿元，在同业中位居前列。实现经营利润26.5亿元，账面税前利润8.15亿元，比上年增加2.03亿元，增长33.3%。

### 主要业务持续快速增长

在公司业务方面，遵循“调整政策，创新产品，积极营销，建设队伍”的发展策略，坚持以客户为中心，加大了对全国性、集团性优质大中型客户的综合营销，加大了总、分（支）行直接营销和联动营销，积极推销网上银行业务，适时为客户提供差异性产品服务，有效提升了本行在同业的市场份额和综合竞争实力。在私人业务方面，按照“完善基础、培育特色、加快发展”的工作思路，加快建立以客户为中心，以服务为宗旨的对私业务综合营销体制，积极开展了对私业务的ISO9001质量体系认证工作，逐步强化对私业务的规范化和标准化建设，有效促进了私人业务的全面发展。在国际业务方面，加大产品创新和市场营销力度，改进对国际结算产品的管理模式，严格控制潜在风险，单证、汇款、保函、保理等国际业务持续稳定增长。在资金业务方面，加强流动性管理，在应对政策调整，市场变化，确保支付安全的同时，积极开拓市场融资渠道，及时调整内部资金价格，创新资金产品，进一步优化债券投资的品种结构、利率结构和期限结构，不仅有效防范了利率波动带来的市场风险，而且也提高了投资收益。

#### 盈利能力进一步提升

2003年，本行把各项经营管理工作的落脚点放到提高效率、增加效益上来，进一步优化网点布局，挖掘网点潜力，提高网点的运营能力和产出效率；高度重视和全力做好信贷工作，狠抓贷款早投放、早收益，加强利率管理，加大对表内表外利息的清收力度，提高贷款综合收息率；大力发展国际结算、网上银行结算、承兑汇票、保理、基金托管等中间业务，拓宽收入来源，非利息收入占比进一步提高；严格控制成本支出，改革用车制度，加强固定资产管理，大力推行报账制，实行财务集中管理，加强费用监督检查。通过多层次、多渠道的增收节支，整体盈利能力有了大幅度提高。

#### 资产质量继续提高

2003年，在风险控制方面，本行积极开展信贷风险项目建设，推进信贷管理体制改革。总行成立了信贷政策委员会，重新调整信贷审批委员会；重组了信贷管理部门，成立了信贷审批部和信贷风险控制部；在华南地区推行了信贷风险管理项目改革试点，信贷管理工作转向垂直化管理，成立华南信贷审批中心，华南片七个分（支）行成立信贷风险控制部；在各分（支）行成立放款中心，严格了贷款操作；改革和完善统一授信制度，信贷风险评估方法；加强对贷款五级分类和异地授信的管理与考核，进一步加大现场和非现场检查监督，建立和推行不良贷款公示制度，责任追究制度和内部计提呆账准备金制度，信贷风险控制与管理能力得到进一步提高。在风险化解方面，进一步加大了对不良资产清收考核力度，加强对抵债资产的管理与处置工作，总行成立了不良资产处置委员会，对全行在海南的不良资产进行集中公开拍卖，为集中处置探索了新的经验。截止到2003年末，全行共现金清收回“两呆”或“可疑、损失”类不良资产30.86亿元，按四级分类的不良资产和不良贷款余额分别比年初减少75亿元和51.6亿元，不良资产率和不良贷款率分别为7.42%和7.5%。

<div style="text-align: center;"><img src="imgs/img_in_image_box_175_1100_993_1375.jpg" alt="Image" width="68%" /></div>


## 新产品开发与信息系统建设取得新进展

2003年，本行先后开发推出了开放式基金的托管和代销、代理外资银行资金清算、代理信托计划资金收付、阳光国际旅行卡、外币结构性存款、QFII资金托管、境内居民个人售汇、资产转让、仓单质押贷款等31项新业务、新产品，增强了服务功能，满足了市场需求，促进了业务发展。在电子化建设中，牢固树立科技兴行的观念，进一步优化和完善综合柜台系统、阳光卡系统、外汇买卖系统、国际结算系统、网上银行等；开发上线了阳光国际旅行卡系统、阳光卡城市前置系统、开放式基金代销系统、财务费用管理系统、视频会议系统等；启动了新一代核心业务项目、阳光贷记卡项目、信贷风险管理项目、系统灾备项目等重点项目建设工程；加强了网络系统的安全管理与技术维护工作，确保业务处理和管理信息系统的通畅安全。2003年的电子化建设，是近几年来投入最大、成果最多的一年，有力地提高了本行的核心竞争力。

### 队伍建设和发展

2003年，本行经营班子进行了调整充实，分行、直属支行经营班子建设得到加强。建立了系统工会委员会，加大了员工的职业技术教育培训工作。在全行广泛开展了“整顿工作纪律，整顿工作作风，提高工作效率，提高经营管理水平”的“双整顿、双提高”活动，提高了全行员工的风险意识和危机意识；提高了遵章守纪，从严治行的认识；提高了苦练内功、向管理要效益的认识。这对于本行加强内控建设，坚持依法合规经营，改进工作作风，提高工作效率、有效防范风险，起到了十分积极的作用。

<div style="text-align: center;"><img src="imgs/img_in_image_box_271_1102_1076_1369.jpg" alt="Image" width="67%" /></div>


# SENIOR MANAGEMENT REPORT

In year two thousand and three, China Everbright Bank (the Bank) realized coordinated development in asset scale, quality and profitability, fulfilling and over-performing those business targets and tasks set forth by the board of directors at year beginning. By the end of year 2003, the Bank's total assets reached RMB394.4 billion yuan, an increase of RMB75.2 billion yuan and up 23.6% over that of the year beginning. Total outstanding loans reached RMB248.6 billion yuan, an increase of RMB57.3 billion yuan and up 30% over that of year beginning, out of which individual loans reached RMB20.4 billion yuan, an increase of RBM11.5 billion yuan and up 130%; total corporate and savings deposits reached RMB343.9 billion yuan, an increase of RMB75.7 billion yuan and up 28.2%, out of which savings deposits reached RMB37.5 billion yuan, an increase of RMB 11.5 billion yuan and up 44.2% over that of the year beginning. The proportion of savings deposits to total corporate and savings deposits was increased from 9.7% to 10.9%. Total Suncard issuance reached RMB12.67 million, an increase of RMB2.64 million and up 26.4% over that of the year beginning, while average Sunccard deposit balance reached RMB1,097 yuan, an increase of RMB397 yuan over that of the previous year. Total international settlement reached USD20 billion, an increase of USD5 billion and up 33% over that of the previous year. Net fee income from international settlement reached RMB192 million yuan, up 22.8%. Total factoring business exceeded RMB10 billion yuan, ranking among the top in domestic peer banks. Total pre-provisioning income reached RMB2.65 billion yuan. Total pre-taxation profits reached RMB815 million yuan, an increase of RMB203 billion yuan and up 33.3% over that of the previous year.

#### CONTINUED RAPID DEVELOPMENT IN MAJOR BUSINESS SECTORS

In corporate banking sector, following the development guidelines of "policy rectification, product innovation, proactive marketing and professional team build-up", the Bank adhered to the customer-oriented focus, put more emphasis on the marketing of national and group client of large or medium size, and consolidated marketing efforts from the head office to branches. In addition to promoting e-banking services and providing tailored products for various clients, the Bank successfully raised its market shares and overall competitiveness. In retail banking sector, following the guidelines of "basis facilitation, uniqueness build-up and development acceleration", the Bank speed up its pace in establishing a customer-oriented and services-focused retail sector marketing system. Moreover, the Bank further introduced ISO9001 certification process for its retail banking business, gradually standardized its retail banking services, and effectively promoted the all-round development in retail banking sector. In international settlement sector, the bank mobilized more efforts in product innovation and marketing, improved management and operation on international settlement products, exerted effective risk management, and ensured continued and stable development in its international business including documentary credit and collection, telegraphic transfer, guarantee, factoring and etc.. In treasury sector, while enhancing liquidity management, effectively reacting to policy and market changes, and ensuring payment capability, the Bank further explores funding sources

and adjusted internal fund transfer pricing wherever and whenever necessary. Through constant treasury product innovation and optimization of bond investment portfolio, interest rate structure and maturity structure, the Bank was able to best mitigate market risks due to interest rate fluctuation and maximize its investment return.

##### FURTHER ELEVATION IN PROFITABILITY

In year two thousand and three, the Bank focused its operation and management tasks on raising efficiency and profitability, further optimizing branch network layout, best exploring network potentials, and facilitating the capability and efficiency of all banking offices. Moreover, the Bank spared no efforts in improving loan management, emphasizing the concept of early loan granting with early return, exert effective interest rate management, and engaged more efforts in interest receivables collection so as to maximize overall interest recovery ratio. The Bank also concentrate more resources in developing fee-based businesses including international settlement, online clearing services, acceptance bills, factoring, and fund custody services so as to multiply its income spectrum. As a result, the Bank's fee income ratio has been successfully elevated. In addition to all these efforts, the Bank exerted strict cost and expense management, conducted reform in corporate car using mechanism, improved fixed asset management, implemented centralized financial management, and strengthened supervision and inspection on expense control. Through all these measures in raising income and controlling expenses at various levels and channels, the Bank has substantially raised its profitability.

#### CONTINUOUS IMPROVEMENT IN ASSET QUALITY

In year two thousand and three, the Bank proactively implemented credit risk management project and furthered its reform in credit management system. Within the head office, Credit Policy Committee was established, while adjustment was made to the Credit Approval Committee. The former Credit Management Department was restructured into two function units, namely Credit Examination and Approval Department and Credit Risk Control Department. Pioneer program in risk management reform was implemented at those branches in south China region, as a result of which vertical credit management was being adopted. South China Credit Examination and Approval Center was established, and Credit Risk Control Department was set up respectively in the seven branches in south China region. Furthermore, Loan Releasing Center

<div style="text-align: center;"><img src="imgs/img_in_image_box_261_1220_1079_1504.jpg" alt="Image" width="68%" /></div>


was established in all branches so as to regulate loan operations. The Bank also conducted centralized credit management reform and further improved credit risk analysis methodology. Through further implementing effective management and evaluation in five-category loan classification standard and inter-city credit approval for loan business, enhancing on-site and off-site inspection, establishing rational mechanism in NPL disclosure, personnel punishment, and bad loan provision, the Bank further improved credit risk management capabilities. As for bad asset workout, the Bank strengthened compensation system in non-performing asset recovery, enhanced settled asset management by setting up Non-performing Asset Disposal Committee. By holding public auction for non-performing assets in Hainan province, the Bank was able to accrue new experience in block disposal of non-performing assets. By the end of year two thousand and three, for those bad assets classified as "non-performing" and "bad", or "doubtful" and "loss", the Bank realized cash recovery of RMB3,086 million yuan. The NPA and NPL balance according to the four-category classification standard have respectively dropped by RMB7.5 billion yuan and RMB5.16 billion yuan, while the NPA ratio and NPL ratio were respectively brought down to 7.42% and 7.5%.

##### NEW PROGRESS IN PRODUCT INNOVATION AND IT IMPLEMENTATION

In year two thousand and three, the Bank has developed and implemented 31 new products and services including mutual fund custody and distribution, foreign bank clearing services, trust investment settlement services, suncard for international travel, foreign currency structured deposit, QFII fund custody, foreign currency selling services for domestic individuals, asset transfer, cargo receipt pledged loans, and etc.. Consequently, the Bank enriched its product mix in light of market demand and achieved favorable business development. In the aspect of IT implementation, following the ideology of building competitiveness with advance technology, the Bank improved its frontier system, suncard system, foreign exchange dealing system, international settlement system and e-banking system. Moreover, the bank developed and implemented

<div style="text-align: center;"><img src="imgs/img_in_image_box_177_1094_600_1352.jpg" alt="Image" width="35%" /></div>


<div style="text-align: center;"><img src="imgs/img_in_image_box_613_1093_992_1352.jpg" alt="Image" width="31%" /></div>


international travel suncard system, suncard frontline system, open-end mutual fund distribution system, financial management system and visual meeting system. The Bank also initiated various key IT projects including new core banking system, credit card system, credit risk management system and disaster backup system. In addition to all these efforts, the Bank further strengthened security management and technical maintenance for its entire computer network so as to ensure the smooth operation and security for business transactions and management information system. In fact, the Bank mobilized more resources than ever for IT implementation in 2003 and was therefore rewarded with substantial elevation in core competitiveness.

#### STEADY IMPROVEMENT IN FACULTY TEAM BUILD-UP AND CORPORATE CULTURE

In year two thousand and three, the Bank's senior management and branch management team were further optimized and enhanced. In order to best protect employee interests, the Bank established labor union committee, put more resources in professional training and education. More importantly, the Bank extensively carried out "dual consolidation and dual improvement" program, which is focused on consolidating working discipline, consolidating working professionalism, improving efficiency and improving business management and operation. As a result of such a program, the Bank was able to convene consensus in strengthening risk management, acknowledging its own weaknesses, abiding by internal rules and regulation, managing the Bank with strictest discipline, and seeking profitability via better management. As a result, the program has played positive role in strengthening internal control, ensuring compliant operation, fostering professionalism, raising efficiency and mitigating business risks.

<div style="text-align: center;"><img src="imgs/img_in_image_box_261_1093_1074_1355.jpg" alt="Image" width="68%" /></div>


## 公司治理情况

##### 概述

2003年是本行公司治理发生明显变化、取得积极进展的一年。本行在原有公司治理框架的基础上，按照监管部门的要求，积极开展制度创新，加快改进公司治理的步伐，维护全体股东和其他利益相关者的合法权益，促进了银行稳健经营和健康发展。

2003年，本行从健全规章制度入手，进一步完善公司治理。年初按照《股份制商业银行公司治理指引》等法律法规的要求修订了银行章程；制订了股东大会、董事会、监事会的议事规则和董事会及监事会各专门委员会的工作规则；年中又根据银监会的意见和实践要求，对本行章程和有关议事规则及工作规则的部分条款作了进一步的修改和补充；并先后制订了本行高级管理层工作规则、高级管理层向董事会报告与报批重大经营管理事项暂行办法、董事会授权董事长在董事会闭会期间行使若干职权的制度、董事会向行长授权的制度、监事行为准则和监事会审计操作程序等十几项重要规章制度。这些制度和办法的出台，进一步规范了股东大会、董事会、监事会和高级管理层的工作行为、职能划分和运作程序，从制度上促进了决策、执行和监督三位一体、互相制约的治理架构和分权、分责、制衡的公司治理机制的初步形成。

<div style="text-align: center;"><img src="imgs/img_in_image_box_168_899_588_1165.jpg" alt="Image" width="35%" /></div>


<div style="text-align: center;"><img src="imgs/img_in_image_box_600_900_993_1161.jpg" alt="Image" width="32%" /></div>


在过去一年里，董事会对重大事项的决策职能得到强化。本行董事会聘任了两名独立董事，正式设立了提名委员会、薪酬委员会、风险管理委员会和审计与关联交易控制委员会。各专门委员会分别审议了高级管理人员的提名程序，增加独立董事人选的建议、董事和监事的年费标准、高级管理人员薪酬与绩效评价的原则意见、风险管理、重大关联交易等一系列重要事项，进一步发挥了董事会的科学决策作用。董事会切实履行职责，除按季召开四次例会外，还根据工作需要召开临时会议，先后讨论了四十多项议题。

监事会的监督职能得到进一步加强。本行监事会聘任了两名外部监事，设立了提名委员会和审

计委员会两个专门委员会，提升了监事会检查和监督本行财务活动的能力和水平。2003年，监事会审议通过十几项议案，审计委员会还专门组织实施了对本行固定资产管理情况的专项审计，促进了本行改进固定资产管理工作。同时，监事会会同有关方面对本行的票据业务进行了检查。

信息披露工作进一步加强。本行按照《商业银行信息披露暂行办法》的要求，进一步充实和规范了年报内容；2003年召开了两次股东通报会，通报银监会2002年度监管通报的内容，解答股东提出的各项问题；编写《董、监事会通讯》，寄送全体股东单位，使股东们比较及时地了解银行公司治理和经营管理的信息，不断加强与股东和董、监事经常性的信息交流与沟通。

### 报告期内召开股东大会情况

2003年1月16日，本行2003年临时股东大会在北京举行。出席本次股东大会并进行投票表决的股东代表共计79人，以总股本82.1689亿股计算，代表股份60.732亿股，占本行总股本的73.9%，符合《中华人民共和国公司法》和《中国光大银行章程》的有关规定。会议以记名投票表决方式审议通过以下议案：1、《中国光大银行股份有限公司章程》2003年修订稿；2、《中国光大银行股份有限公司股东大会议事规则》；3、《中国光大银行股份有限公司董事会议事规则》；4、《中国光大银行股份有限公司监事会议事规则》；5、《中国光大银行股份有限公司关于设立独立董事和独立董事选聘程序的议案》；6、《中国光大银行股份有限公司关于设立外部监事和外部监事选聘程序的议案》。

2003年7月11日，本行第七次股东大会（即2003年年度股东大会）在北京召开。出席本次股东大会并进行投票表决的股东代表共计97人，以银行总股本82.1689亿股计算，代表股份66.41亿股，占银行总股本的80.82%，符合《中华人民共和国公司法》和《中国光大银行股份有限公司章程》的有关规定。会议以记名投票表决的方式审议通过了如下报告和议案：1、《中国光大银行2002年度董事会工作报告》；2、《中国光大银行2002年度监事会工作报告》；3、《中国光大银行2002年度财务决算和2003年度财务预算的报告》；4、《中国光大银行2002年度利润分配预案》；5、《关于选举中国光大银行第三届董事会成员的议案》；6、《关于选举中国光大银行第三届监事会成员的议案》；7、《关于授权董事会根据银监会意见对本行章程若干条款进行修订的议案》。

### 报告期内董、监事更换情况

2003 年 3 月 7 日，本行股东大会以通讯表决的形式审议通过了关于聘任夏斌先生和 Steven W. Hoagland 先生为本行独立董事、聘任钟苏汉先生为本行监事的提案，刘应富先生不再担任中国光大银行监事之职。

根据章程规定，本行董事会和监事会每届任期三年。2003年，本行董、监事会换届选举。2003年7月11日，本行第七次股东大会选举王明权、王川、郭友、武青、姜波、单建保、李光林、

陈觉民，Jonghwa Lee（李钟化）、马建平，董晓云，张镇德，郭明，夏斌，Steven W. Hoagland（史蒂文·霍克兰德）为本行第三届董事会董事；同时，股东大会还选举陈铁林，王玉贵，叶逊，李兆会，郑中安，任侠先生和鲍国明女士为本行第三届监事会监事，与职工民主选举产生的职工监事杨俊川，郭庆卫，陈昱，王延新共同组成第三届监事会。

word-wrap: break-word;'>董事长</td></tr><tr><td style='text-align: center; word-wrap: break-word;'>夏斌</td><td style='text-align: center; word-wrap: break-word;'>男</td><td style='text-align: center; word-wrap: break-word;'>53</td><td style='text-align: center; Hoagland</td><td style='text-align: center; word-wrap: break-word;'>男</td><td style='text-align: center; word-wrap: break-word;'>55</td><td style='text-align: center; word-wrap: break-word;'>独立咨询顾问（美国）</td><td style='text-align: center; word-wrap: break-word;'>独立董事</td></tr></table>

[注] 2004年2月17日本行召开临时股东大会，审议通过吴明华先生为本行独立董事。

#### 董事在本行领取薪酬情况

独立董事和管理层董事在本行领取薪酬，董事长和其他董事不在本行领取薪酬。全部董事均不持有本行股份。

##### 报告期内董事会及专门委员会会议情况

2003年1月16日，本行第二届董事会第十次会议在北京召开，出席会议的董事9人，委托行使表决权的董事4人，会议合法有效。会议审议通过了如下报告和议案：1、《中国光大银行关于聘任夏斌先生和Hoagland博士为本行独立董事的提案》；2、《中国光大银行关于聘任陈元生先生为董事会秘书的提案》；3、《关于提请董事会审核部分股东转让股份及受让方股东资格的报告》。

2003年4月17日，本行第二届董事会第十一次会议在北京召开，出席会议的董事9人，委托行使表决权的董事4人，会议合法有效。会议审议通过了如下报告和议案：1、《中国光大银行2002年度董事会工作报告》；2、《中国光大银行2002年度经营管理工作报告》；3、《中国光大银行2002年度财务决算和2003年度财务预算的报告》；4、《中国光大银行2002年度利润分配预案》；5、《中国光大银行2002年年度报告》；6、《中国光大银行关于董事会换届提名方案中董事名额调整的议案》；7、《中国光大银行关于第三届董事会董事候选人名单的议案》；8、《中国光大银行关于聘请律师见证股东大会的议案》。

2003年7月11日，本行第三届董事会第一次会议在北京召开，出席会议的董事11人，委托行使表决权的董事3人，会议合法有效。会议审议通过了如下报告和议案：1、《关于选举王明权先生为中国光大银行第三届董事会董事长、选举王川先生为副董事长的议案》；2、《关于聘任武青先生、李杰女士为中国光大银行副行长的议案》；3、《关于成立中国光大银行董事会专门委员会和组成人员建议名单的议案》；4、《关于中国光大银行董事会专门委员会工作暂行规则若干条款修改的议案》；5、《中国光大银行2003年上半年经营情况和下半年工作安排的报告》；6、《关于提请董事会审核部分股东转让股份和受让方股东资格的议案》。

2003年7月24日，本行董事会风险管理委员会第一次会议在北京召开。会议讨论了本行内部风险控制的基本架构，进一步明确了委员会的工作职责，确定了委员会下半年的工作计划，研究了工作小组的组建事宜。

2003年7月24日，本行董事会审计与关联交易控制委员会第一次会议在北京召开。会议讨论了银监会、本行章程和有关规则对委员会工作职责的要求，研究确定了年度工作计划，并讨论了委员会下设工作小组的组建事宜。

2003年7月31日，本行董事会提名委员会第一次会议在北京召开。会议讨论了委员会的工作职责和下半年的工作计划，重点研究了董事会和高级管理层的规模和构成、引进新的独立董事和组建工作小组事宜。

2003年8月20日，本行董事会薪酬委员会第一次会议在北京召开。会议进一步明确了委员会的工作职责，审议、提出董、监事（包括独立董事和外部监事）的年费以及交通补贴的标准，研究确定了下半年的工作计划。

2003年10月14日，本行董事会提名委员会第二次会议在上海召开。会议讨论了银监会唐双宁副主席在股份制商业银行监管会议上讲话的有关精神，分析了当前董事会的构成，提出了优化董事会构成的建议，通过了提名委员会的工作程序以及拟提交董事会审议的关于增加1－2名独立董事的提案。

2003年10月15日，本行第三届董事会第二次会议在上海召开。出席会议的董事12人，委托行使表决权的董事3人，会议合法有效。会议审议通过了如下报告和议案：1、《关于增加独立董事并相应修改章程有关条款的议案》；2、《关于中国光大银行信贷风险管理组织架构改革方案的议案》；3、《关于提请董事会审核部分股东转让股份和受让方股东资格的议案》。会议还审议了《中国光大银行高级管理层向董事会报告和报批重大经营管理事项暂行办法》、《中国光大银行董事会关于授权董事长在董事会闭会期间行使董事会若干职权的议案》以及《中国光大银行高级管理层工作规则》，会后于2003年11月26日经董事会通讯表决通过。

2003年11月3日，本行董事会审计与关联交易控制委员会第二次会议在昆明召开，会议审议了关于对光大集团及其关联企业核定最高授信额度问题、关于本行继续为光大集团提供信用担保问题以及光大集团在本行2002年度分红款的使用问题。

2003年12月15日，本行董事会薪酬委员会第二次会议在北京召开。会议审议通过了关于调整董、监事交通补贴标准的议案，提出了关于完善高级管理人员薪酬制度的原则意见，并讨论了高级管理层提出的关于按照超额利润的一定比例计提奖励问题。

2003年12月16日，本行第三届董事会第三次会议在北京召开。出席会议的董事11人，委托行使表决权的董事4人，会议合法有效。会议审议通过了《关于申请追加2003年度呆帐核销计划的提案》、《关于对光大集团及其关联企业核定83.11亿元人民币最高授信额度的议案》，并原则同意在2004年度发行次级定期债务和进行增资募股两项提高资本充足率的措施。

### 报告期内对股东大会决议的执行情况

在报告期内，根据股东大会的决议，第三届董事会和第三届监事会正式成立，分别选举产生了董事长、副董事长和监事会主席。会后，本行根据有关规定向中国银行业监督管理委员会申报了新任董、监事的任职资格。

2003年下半年，董事会根据股东大会通过的利润分配方案，组织实施了分红工作，按每股0.04元向全体股东（股权被查封或冻结的除外）发放现金红利。董事会还根据股东大会的授权，按照银监会的有关意见并结合本行实际，对本行章程的若干条款进行了必要的修订。

#### 经董事会通过的 2004 年度主要经营策略和目标计划

2004 年主要经营策略是：以 2003 年经营计划及完成情况为基础，以增强可持续发展能力为目标，正确处理好规模、质量、效益的关系，紧紧抓住有效发展、调整资产负债结构、大力化解不良资产等重点工作，努力降低资本占用和风险资产总量，提高资本充足率水平，提高资产质量，全面增强盈利能力，实现不良资产和不良贷款 “双降” 目标，坚持 “安全性、流动性、效益性” 的原则，不断提升股东价值和股本回报率。

2004年主要业务计划为：一般存款达到3950亿元，比上年增长15%；贷款达到2800亿元，增长13%；资产总额达到4600亿元，增长17%；实现账面税前利润10亿元，增长23%；预计股东权益达到136亿元。

##### 监事在本行领取薪酬情况

监事会主席、外部监事、职工监事在本行领取薪酬，其他监事不在本行领取薪酬。全部监事均不持有本行股份。

##### 监事会会议及专门委员会会议情况

2003年1月16日，本行第二届监事会第八次会议于北京召开，出席会议监事11人，委托行使表决权的监事5人。会议审议通过了《关于中国光大银行股份有限公司部分监事变更的提案》；通讯表决通过了《中国光大银行股份有限公司监事会审计委员会工作规则（暂行）》和《中国光大银行股份有限公司监事会提名委员会工作规则（暂行）》。

2003年4月18日，本行第二届监事会第九次会议于北京召开。出席会议监事10人，委托行使表决权的监事6人。会议审议通过了《中国光大银行股份有限公司2002年度会计报表及审计报告》、《中国光大银行监事会2002年度工作报告》、《中国光大银行关于监事会换届提名方案中监事名额调整的议案》、《中国光大银行关于聘任任侠先生和鲍国明女士为外部监事的议案》、《中国光大银行关于第三届监事会股东单位提名监事候选人名单的议案》和《中国光大银行监事行为准则（暂行）》。

2003年7月11日，本行第三届监事会第一次会议于北京召开。出席会议的监事9人。会议审议通过了《关于选举陈铁林先生为中国光大银行第三届监事会主席的议案》、《关于成立中国光大银行监事会专门委员会及组成人员建议名单的议案》和《关于中国光大银行监事会专门委员会工作暂行规则若干条款修改的议案》。

2003年8月1日，本行第三届监事会审计委员会第一次会议于北京召开。出席会议的委员5人。会议审议通过了《关于中国光大银行固定资产管理专项审计方案的议案》。

2003年10月15日，本行第三届监事会第二次会议于上海召开。出席会议的监事7人，委托行使表决权的监事4人。会议审议了《关于制订〈中国光大银行监事会审计操作程序（试行）〉的议案》，通讯表决通过了该议案。

2003年11月28日，本行第三届监事会审计委员会第二次会议于北京召开。出席会议的委员5人。会议审议通过了《关于中国光大银行固定资产管理情况的专项审计报告》。

2003年12月16日，本行第三届监事会第三次会议于北京召开。出席会议的监事8人，委托行使表决权的监事3人。会议审议通过了《关于中国光大银行固定资产管理情况的专项审计报告》。

本报告期内，监事会列席了历次董事会会议。

## 高管人员在本行领取报酬情况

上述高管人员在本行领取报酬共计为 515.5 万元，金额最高的前三名高管人员的报酬总额为 213.6 万元。

<div style="text-align: center;"><img src="imgs/img_in_image_box_195_1204_1076_1496.jpg" alt="Image" width="73%" /></div>


##### OVERVIEW

2003 witnessed CEB's obvious changes and rapid progress in corporate governance. Based on the existing framework of corporate governance, pursuant to the regulator's requirements, CEB spared no efforts in innovating relevant rules, quickened the steps in improving corporate governance, and safeguard the lawful rights of all the shareholders as well as other stakeholders, which contributed to the Bank's stable operation and healthy growth.

In 2003, CEB further improved its corporate governance starting from amplifying rules and regulations. At the beginning of year 2003, in accordance with the regulations such as Guidelines on Corporate Governance for Joint-Stock Commercial Banks, the Bank made substantial amendments to Articles of Association; in second half of the year, the Bank formulated Rules of Procedures for Shareholders Meeting, Rules of Procedures for Board of Directors, Rules of Procedures for Board of Supervisors, Work Rules for Special Committees under the Board of Directors and Board of Supervisors. In the middle of the year, in light of the opinions given by China Banking Regulatory Commission and the needs for business development, the Bank further revised certain articles of the Bank's Articles of Association, Rules of Procedures as well as work rules. The Bank has successively worked out a dozen of important rules and regulations including Work Rules for CEB's Senior Management, Provisional Rules on Senior Management Reporting Major Issues to the Board of Directors, Rules on the Board of Directors Authorizing Chairman to Exercise Partial Rights during Adjournment, Rules for the Board of Directors to Make Authorization to President, Code of Conduct for Supervisors, Procedures of Auditing for the Board of Supervisors, etc. With the formulation of these rules and regulations, work behaviors, function definition and operating procedures for the Shareholder's Meeting, the Board of Directors, the Board of Supervisors and the senior management have been further standardized. As far as rules are concerned, a governance framework of three-in-one with the functional bodies of decision-making, execution and supervision restricting each other has been established. In the meantime, a corporate governance characterized by power separation, responsibilities distribution and check-and-balance mechanism has also been basically set up.

In the past year, Board of Directors has strengthened its function of making decisions on major issues. Two independent directors were appointed to the Board of Directors, and Nomination Committee, Remuneration Committee, Risk Management Committee and Auditing and Related-party Transaction Committee have been formally established. Each special committee has respectively examined such issues as Procedures of Nominating Senior Management, Proposal to Increase the Number of Independent Directors, Standard of Annual Compensation for Directors and Supervisors, Principles of Remuneration and Performance Appraisal for Senior Management, rules on risk management and major related-party transactions. As a result, the role played by the Board of Directors in making decisions reasonably has been significantly intensified. To fulfill its responsibilities, the Board of Directors sometimes holds provisional meeting in between quarterly

routine meetings when necessary, at which more than forty proposals were discussed successively.

The supervising function of the Board of Supervisors has been further strengthened. Two external supervisors were nominated, Nomination Committee and Auditing Committee were set up so that the capability of the Board of Supervisors in conducting inspection and supervision over financial activities was promoted. In 2003, a dozen of proposals were approved by the Board of Supervisors, the Auditing Committee carried out special auditing over the management of the Bank's fixed assets, leading to an improvement in the Bank's management of fixed assets. In addition to that, the Board of Supervisors along with other departments concerned made a general inspection over the Bank's bill business.

Further progress has been made in information disclosure. Pursuant to the requirements of Provisional Rues on Information Disclosure for Commercial Banks, more detailed information were disclosed in the Bank's annual report. Two shareholders' meetings were convened in 2003 to report the inspection results for 2002 given by China Banking Regulatory Commission, and answering the questions raised by shareholders. A Circular for the Board of Directors and Board of Supervisors was drafted and delivered to all the shareholders, which enabled shareholders to be timely informed of the Bank's progress in corporate governance and operation performance. In that way, information exchange and communication among the shareholders, directors and supervisors have been enhanced.

##### SHAREHOLDERS' MEETINGS DURING THE REPORTING PERIOD

On 16 January 2003, the interim General Meeting of Shareholders was held in Beijing. Seventy nine shareholders attended and exercised the right to vote at the meeting. The shareholders present represented 6073.2 million shares of the Bank, accounting for 73.9% of the total stock shares, based on total 8216.89 million shares, which was in compliance with the Company Law of China and the Article of Association of the Bank. The meeting reviewed and approved the following proposals: 1. Article of Association of the Bank (revised version 2003); 2. Rules of Procedure for the General Meetings of Shareholders of CEB; 3. Rules of Procedure for the Board of Directors of CEB; 4. Rules of Procedure for the Board of Supervisors of CEB; 5. Proposal to Appoint Independent Directors and Appointment Procedure; 6. Proposal to Appoint External Supervisors and Appointment Procedure.

On 11 July 2003, the seventh Shareholders' Meeting (Shareholders' Annual Meeting 2003) was held in Beijing. Ninety seven shareholders attended and exercised the right to vote at the meeting. The shareholders present represented 6641 million shares of the Bank, accounting for 80.82% of the total stock shares (based on a total of 8216.89 million shares) of the Bank, which complied with the Company Law of China and the Article of Association of the Bank. The following reports and proposals were reviewed and approved: 1. Work Report by the Board of Directors for 2002; 2. Work Report of the Board of Supervisors for 2002; 3. CEB's Financial

Report for 2002 and Financial Projection for 2003; 4. CEB's Scheme of Profit Appropriation for 2002; 5. Proposal to Elect Directors of CEB's Third Board of Directors; 6. Proposal to Elect Supervisors of CEB's Third Board of Supervisors; 7. Proposal to Authorize the Board of Directors to Revise Certain Articles of the CEB's Articles of Association Pursuant to the Opinions of China Banking Regulatory Commission.

#### REPLACEMENT OF DIRECTORS AND SUPERVISORS DURING THE REPORTING PERIOD

On 7 March 2003, by voting via communication, the Shareholders meeting appointed Mr. Xia Bin and Dr. Steven W. Hoagland independent directors of the Bank, and appointed Mr. Zhong Suhan the supervisor of the Bank, taking the place of Mr. Liu Yingfu.

According to the Articles of Association, the Bank's directors and supervisors are elected every three years. Election of the directors and supervisors was carried out in 2003. On 11 July 2003, Mr. Wang Mingquan, Mr. Wang Chuan, Mr. Guo You, Mr. Wu Qing, Mrs. Jiang Bo, Mr. Shan Jianbao, Mr. Li Guanglin, Mr. Chen Juemin, Mr. Jonghwa Lee, Mr. Ma Jianping, Mr. Dong Xiaoyun, Mr. Zhang Zhende, Mr. Guo Ming, Mr. Xia Bin, Dr. Steven W Hoagland were formally elected directors of the Bank's Third Board of Directors at the Seventh Shareholders' Conference. In addition, Mr. Chen Tielin, Mr. Wang Yugui, Mr. Ye Xun, Mr. Li Zhaohui, Mr. Zheng Zhongan, Mr. Ren Xia and Ms. Bao Guoming were elected supervisors of the Bank's Third Board of Supervisors. Mr. Yang Junchuan, Mr. Guo Qingwei, Mr. Chen Yu and Mr. Wang Yanxin were elected supervisors from the staff members.

##### BANK DIRECTORS' REMUNERATION

Independent directors and management member directors get remuneration from the Bank. The Chairman and other directors do not receive remuneration from the Bank. No Board Director holds shares of the Bank.

### MEETINGS OF THE BOARD OF DIRECTORS AND THE SPECIAL COMMITTEES IN THE REPORTING PERIOD

On 16 January 2003, the Tenth Meeting of the Second Board of Directors was held in Beijing. Nine directors were present at the meeting, four directors entrusted other directors to exercise the right to vote. The meeting was legal and effective. The following reports and proposals were reviewed and approved: 1. Proposal to Appoint Mr. Xia Bin and Dr. Steven Hoagland as CEB's Independent Directors; 2. Proposal to Appoint Mr. Chen Yuansheng as Secretary of CEB's Board of Directors; 3. Report to the Board of Directors for Examination of the Share Transfer by Some Shareholders and Verification of the Transferee's Qualification. On 17 April 2003, the eleventh meeting of the second Board of Directors was held in Beijing. Nine directors

attended the meeting and four directors entrusted other directors to exercise voting right. The meeting was legal and effective. Following reports and proposals were reviewed and approved at this meeting:

1 Report of CEB's Board of Directors for Year 2002;

2 Report on Business Operation and Management of CEB for Year 2002;

3 Financial Report for 2002 and Financial Projection for 2003;

4 Scheme of Profit Appropriation for Year 2002 to be submitted to the Shareholders' Meeting for approval;

5 Annual Report of CEB for Year 2002;

6 Proposal to Change the Number of Directors on the New Board of Directors;

7 Proposal on the Candidates for Directors on CEB's Third Board of Directors; and

8 Proposal to Inviting Lawyers to Witness the Shareholders' Meeting.

On 11 July 2003, the first meeting of the third Board of Directors was held in Beijing. Eleven directors attended the meeting and three directors entrusted other directors to exercise voting right. The meeting was legal and effective. The following reports and proposals were reviewed and approved at this meeting:

1 Proposal to Elect Mr. Wang Mingquan as Chairman and Elect Mr. Wang Chuan as Vice Chairman of CEB's Third Board of Directors;

2 Proposal to Appoint Mr. Wu Qing and Mrs. Li Jie as Executive Vice President of CEB;

3 Proposal on the Establishment of Special Committees under the Board of Directors and the Name List of the Proposed Members;

4 Proposal on Amendments to the Provisional Work Rules for Special Committees under the Board of Directors;

5 Report on CEB's Business Operation of the First Half of 2003 and the Work Plan for the Second Half of 2003; and

6 Proposal on Share Transfers by Some Shareholders to be Submitted to the Board of Directors for Approval and the Verification of the Transferees' Qualification.

At the first meeting of the Risk Management Committee under the Board of Directors held in Beijing on 24 July 2003, the committee members discussed the basic structure of the internal risk control of CEB, further defined the duties of the committee, clarified the committee's work plan for the second half of the year and reviewed the plan to establish its work team.

On 24 July 2003, the first meeting of the Audit and Related-Party Transactions Committee under the Board of Directors was held in Beijing. At the meeting, the committee reviewed the requirements of CBRC, CEB's Articles of Association and relevant regulation on the committee's functions and duties, adopted its annual work plan and reviewed the matters of establishing its work team.

On 31 July 2003, the first meeting of the Nomination Committee under the Board of Directors was held in Beijing. At this meeting, the committee members reviewed the committee's functions and the work plan for the second half of the year, in particular, discussed the membership and composition of both the Board of Directors and Senior Management, and the issues on introducing new independent directors and establishment of its work team.

On 20 August 2003, the first meeting of the Remuneration Committee under the Board of Directors was held in Beijing. At the meeting, the committee further confirmed its functions, reviewed and proposed the standard for the annual remuneration and transportation allowance for the directors and supervisors (including independent directors and external supervisors as well). In addition, the committee mapped out its work plan for the second half of the year.

On 14 October 2003, the second meeting of the Nomination Committee was convened in Shanghai. At the meeting, the committee reviewed and spirit of the speech delivered by Mr. Tang Shuangning, Vice Chairman of CBRC, at the Meeting of Supervision on Joint-stock Commercial Banks, analyzed the current composition of the Board of Directors and worked out a proposal for optimizing the composition. Besides, the committee approved the rules of procedure of the committee and the proposal on appointing one or two more independent directors, which was to be submitted to the Board of Directors for approval.

On 15 October 2003, the second meeting of the third Board of Directors was held in Shanghai. Twelve directors attended the meeting and three directors entrusted other directors to exercise voting right. The meeting was legal and valid. The following reports and proposals were reviewed and approved at this meeting:

(1) Proposal to Appoint More Independent Directors and Amend CEB's Articles of Association;

(2) Proposal to Reform the Organization Framework of CEB's Credit Risk Management; and

(3) Proposal on Share Transfer Transactions by Some Shareholders to be Submitted to the Board of Directors for Approval and Verification of the Transferees' Qualification.

Moreover, the directors reviewed the Provisional Rules for the Senior Management to Report Major Issues Concerning Business Operation and Management to the Board of Directors; Proposal for Board of Directors to Authorize the Chairman to Exercise Partial Powers during Adjournment and Work Rules of CEB's Senior Management. These proposals were all approved by the Board of Directors via telecom vote on 26 October 2003.

On 3 November 2003, the second meeting of the Audit and Related-Party Transactions Committee under the Board of Directors was held in Kunming. The Committee discussed the issues on assessing the ceiling of the credit line for China Everbright Group and its related parties, CEB continuously providing guarantee on credit for China Everbright Group and the use of the dividend for year 2002 of China Everbright Group.

On 15 December 2003, the second meeting of the Remuneration Committee under the Board of Directors was held in Beijing. The Committee reviewed and approved the Proposal on Adjustment of the Standard of

Transportation Allowance for Directors and Supervisors, proposed Opinions on Improving Senior Management Members' Remuneration System, and discussed the issue raised by the senior management of providing for bonus from the over fulfilled profits.

On 16 December 2003, the third meeting of the third Board of Directors was held in Beijing. Eleven directors were present and four directors entrusted other directors to exercise voting right. The meeting was legal and valid. At the meeting, the directors discussed and approved the Motion on Increasing the Volume of Bad Loan Write-off for Year 2003, the Proposal to Grant RMB8.311 billion yuan as the Ceiling of Credit Line for China Everbright Group and Its Related Parties and approved two proposals to raise capital adequacy ratio by way of issuing subordinate debts and undertaking private placement in year 2004.

## IMPLEMENTATION OF THE RESOLUTIONS OF SHAREHOLDERS' MEETING DURING THE REPORTING PERIOD

In the reporting period, according to the resolutions of the shareholders' meeting, the third Board of Directors and the third Supervisory Board were founded, and the Chairman, Vice Chairman of the Board of Directors and the Chairman of the Supervisory Board were elected respectively. CEB declared the qualification of newly appointed directors and supervisors to CBRC in light of the relevant regulations.

In the second half of 2003, according to the resolutions of the shareholders' meeting with regard to the profit distribution, the Board of Directors gave out dividends to all the shareholders (except those whose equities were blocked or frozen) for RMB0.04 yuan per share. In addition, pursuant to the authorization of the shareholders' meeting and relevant regulations of CBRC, the Board of Directors also made necessary modifications to several terms of CEB's Articles of Association.

## BUSINESS STRATEGIES AND OBJECTIVES FOR YEAR 2004 ADOPTED BY THE BOARD OF DIRECTORS

The core business strategies for year 2004 are: taking the operating results of year 2003 as the basis, aiming at strengthening capability of sustainable development, conciliating the relationship between scale, quality and efficiency, paying great attention to efficient development, adjusting structure of asset and liability and NPL workout, endeavoring to decrease capital use and risk assets, improving capital adequacy level, enhancing asset quality, raising overall profitability, realizing the goal of "double deduction" in both non-performing assets and non-performing loans, insisting on the principles of "safety, liquidity and profitability", and, keeping on enhancing shareholders' value and ROE ratio.

The main business goals for 2004 are: total corporate and savings deposits reach RMB395 billion yuan, growing by 15% compared with the previous year; total outstanding loans reach RMB280 billion yuan, increasing by 13%; total assets amount to RMB460 billion yuan, increasing by 17%; pre-taxation profits reach RMB1 billion yuan, increasing by 23%; and shareholders' equity expected to reach RMB13.6 billion yuan.

## 2. Remuneration of CEB's supervisors

Chairman of the Supervisory Board, the employee supervisors and external supervisors get remuneration from CEB, other supervisors do not get remuneration from CEB. No supervisor holds shares of the Bank.

##### MEETINGS OF THE SUPERVISORY BOARD AND THE SPECIAL COMMITTEES

On 16 January 2003, the eighth meeting of the second Supervisory Board was held in Beijing. Eleven supervisors were present and five entrusted other supervisors to exercise voting right. The supervisors reviewed and approved Motion on Replacement of Supervisors of CEB; Work Rules of the Audit Committee under the Supervisory Board of CEB (provisional) and Work Rules of the Nomination Committee under the Supervisory Board of CEB (provisional) at the meeting.

On 18 April 2003, the ninth meeting of the second Supervisory Board was convened in Beijing. Ten supervisors were present and six entrusted other supervisors to exercise voting right. The following reports and proposals were reviewed and approved at this meeting: Financial Statement and Audit Report for year

2002; Work Report of the Supervisory Board for year 2002; Proposal to Change the Number of Supervisors Nominated to the New Supervisory Board; Proposal to Appoint Mr. Ren Xia and Mrs. Bao Guoming as External Supervisors of CEB; Proposal on the Name List of the Supervisor Candidates Nominated by Shareholders to CEB's Third Supervisory Board; and Behavior Codes of CEB's Supervisors (provisional).

On 11 July 2003, the first meeting of the third Supervisory Board was convened in Beijing. Nine supervisors were present. The meeting reviewed and approved the following proposals: Proposal to Elect Mr. Chen Tielin as Chairman of CEB's Third Supervisory Board; Proposal to Establish Special Committees under the Supervisory Board and the Name List of the Proposed Members; and Proposal on Amendments to the Provisional Work Rules for Special Committees under the Supervisory Board.

On 1 August 2003, the first meeting of the Audit Committee under the third Supervisory Board was held in Beijing. Five supervisors attended the meeting and reviewed and approved the Proposal on the Special Audit of CEB's Fixed Assets.

On 15 October 2003, the second meeting of the third Supervisory Board was held in Shanghai. Seven supervisors attended the meeting and four entrusted other supervisors to exercise voting right. The supervisors reviewed the Proposal on Stipulating Operating Procedures for Auditing of CEB's Supervisory Board (provisional), which was approved by the Supervisory Board via telecom vote.

On 28 November 2003, he second meeting of the Audit Committee under the third Supervisory Board was held in Beijing. Five members present reviewed and approved the Report on Special Audit of CEB's Fixed Assets Management.

On 16 December 2003, the third meeting of the third Supervisory Board was held in Beijing. Eight supervisors attended the meeting and three entrusted other supervisors to exercise voting right. The meeting reviewed and approved the Report on Special Audit of CEB's Fixed Assets Management.

During the reporting period, the Supervisory Board was present at all meetings of the Board of Directors.

# 股东情况及关联交易情况

#### 股份变动情况

截至到 2003 年末，本行股东总数为 226 家。

股份变动情况如下：

1、宁波大榭申洲服饰有限公司将其持有的本行股份3000万股转让给浙江菲达机电集团有限公司，上海上实（集团）有限公司和上海上实国际贸易（集团）有限公司分别将其持有的本行股份13552万股和762.3万股转让给上海爱建信托投资有限公司，于2003年1月16日经第二届董事会第十次会议审议批准。

2、太原市唐润实业有限公司将其持有的本行股份76002841股全部转让给山西国际电力集团有限公司，大庆高新产业技术开发区总公司将其持有的本行股份1694万股全部转让给大庆高新国有资产运营有限公司，于2003年4月11日经第二届董事会通讯表决审议批准。

3、宁波大榭申洲开发区服饰有限公司将其持有的本行股份4700万股转让给太原市唐润实业有限公司，于2003年5月23日经第二届董事会通讯表决审议批准。

4. 红河卷烟厂将其持有的本行股份5719.67万股全部转让给云南省烟草红河州公司，西藏珠峰摩托车工业公司将其持有的本行2860万股全部转让给福建七匹狼集团有限公司，于2003年7月11日经第三届董事会第一次会议审议批准。

5、北京宣房投资管理公司将其持有的本行股份2200万股全部转让给北京市金正资产投资经营公司，于2003年9月24日经第三届董事会通讯表决审议批准。

6. 深圳市鸿波公用电话有限公司将其持有的本行股份880.106万股全部转让给深圳市鸿波通信投资控股有限公司，上海沪昌特殊钢股份有限公司将其持有的本行股份847万股全部转让给宝钢集团上海五钢公司，于2003年10月15日经第三届董事会第二次会议审议批准。

7、中技贸易股份有限公司将其持有的本行股份3557.4万股全部转让给太原市唐润实业有限公司，于2003年11月26日经第三届董事会通讯表决审议批准。

## 持股在5%以上的股东情况

本行持股在5%以上的股东有两家，分别为中国光大（集团）总公司和中国光大控股有限公司。

1、本行第一大股东——中国光大（集团）总公司成立于1983年5月。中国光大集团是中央管理的国有重点骨干企业，以金融业为核心，主要经营银行、证券、保险、投资管理等业务。目前，光大集团在国内拥有中国光大银行，光大证券有限责任公司和光大永明保险公司等金融机构，也是申银万国证券公司的第一大股东。在香港拥有光大控股、光大科技、光大国际三家上市公司。集团以全资、控股、参股等形式管理着国内60多家企业，充分利用内地与香港市场拓展业务。

2. 本行第二大股东——中国光大控股有限公司（前身为明辉发展有限公司），于1973年在香港联交所上市。经过多年的发展和业务、资产的重组，逐步发展成为一家金融控股公司。该公司主要从事金融业务，包括投资银行业务、证券、期货和外汇经纪业务及保险经纪业务。

### 最大十名股东的关联情况

中国光大(集团)总公司通过中国光大集团有限公司(香港)持有中国光大控股有限公司55.47%的股份。中国烟草总公司云南省公司为玉溪红塔烟草（集团）有限责任公司的上级主管部门，但无股权关系。

### 最大十名股东所持股份的抵押、托管、冻结情况

本行前十大股东中，中国光大（集团）总公司和中国船东互保协会分别将其持有的全部本行股份质押给有关企业和银行。

### 重大关联交易情况

## 贷款情况

中国光大（集团）总公司贷款15笔，年末余额21.64亿元，均为股权质押，五级分类均为次级；

青岛国信实业公司贷款4笔，年末余额3.11亿元，其中1.16亿元存单质押，其余为信用贷款，五级分类为正常；

将军烟草集团有限公司贷款2笔，年末余额1亿元，由中国烟草总公司山东省公司提供担保，五级分类为正常；

山西海鑫钢铁集团有限公司贷款1笔7000万元，提供股权质押，五级分类为正常；

上海光大会展中心有限公司贷款1笔3.8亿元，提供土地抵押，五级分类为关注；

上海光大旅游物业公司贷款1笔300万元，由中国光大旅游总公司提供担保，五级分类为损失；

广州市胜业房地产发展有限公司贷款1笔380万元，由中国光大（集团）总公司提供担保，五级分类为可疑；

顺德市顺通集团有限公司贷款1笔500万元，提供股权质押，五级分类为可疑；

光大木材（深圳）有限公司贷款1笔3450万元，厂房及土地抵押，五级分类为关注；

上述关联交易发生的贷款合计30.71亿元，占全行贷款的1.2%。其中，不良贷款为21.76亿元，占全行不良贷款总额的9.3%，占关联交易总量的27.49%。

## 拆借情况

光大证券有限责任公司拆借3笔，年末余额8.16亿元，其中3.16亿元逾期，5亿元为正常；

光大财务有限公司拆借3笔，年末余额美元15150万元，港币16200万元，均为正常；

上述关联交易发生的拆借合计 22.42 亿元人民币，占全行拆借的 47.91%。

### 存放同业情况

存放光大证券有限责任公司 2 笔，年末余额 7652 万元，已逾期。

上述关联交易发生的存放同业合计7652万元，占全行存放同业的0.84%。

#### 担保情况

为中国光大（集团）总公司提供担保3笔，年末余额24亿元，占全行担保余额的26.9%。

### 进入不良状态的股东贷款情况

中国光大（集团）总公司贷款15笔，年末余额21.64亿元，均为股权质押，五级分类均为次级。进入不良状态的原因是该公司上述贷款都是2000年以前形成的遗留问题，但这些贷款都有股权质押，没有最终风险。

北京中关村科技发展（控股）股份有限公司贷款2笔，年末余额3亿元，五级分类为次级，其中1亿元由北京中关村证券有限公司提供1.4亿股权质押；2亿元由北京中关村开发建设股份有限公司提供全额连带责任担保，同时，其中的1.12亿元又由北京中关村四环医药开发有限公司提供股权质押。进入不良状态的原因是该公司负债过多，投资收益与预期差异较大，资金周转困难。

中国轻骑集团有限公司贷款2笔，年末余额14130万元，五级分类为可疑，其中9300万元由济南轻骑摩托车股份有限公司提供担保，4830万元由济南轻骑铃木摩托车有限公司提供担保。进入不良状态的原因是该公司为原中国投资银行历史遗留问题，经营不善，管理粗放，对市场把握不准。

Shanghai Huchang Special Steel Co., Ltd. transferred 8.47 million shares it held to Shanghai Baosteel Corporation No. 5 Steel Co., Ltd.. China Technology Trade Co., Ltd transferred 35.574 million shares it held to Taiyuan Tangrun Industrial Co., Ltd. The share transfer was approved by the third Board of Directors on November 26, 2003.

#### PROFILE OF THE SHAREHOLDERS HOLDING SHARES ABOVE 5%

There are two shareholders holding shares of the Bank above 5%, which are China Everbright Group Ltd (hereinafter abbreviated as CEG) and China Everbright Limited respectively.

1. The biggest shareholder of the Bank - CEG was established in May 1983. As a leading conglomerate managed by central government, CEG has been operating mainly in such business areas as banking, securities, insurance and investment management with financial industry as its core business. Currently, CEG is the major shareholder of the following financial institutions: China Everbright Bank, China Everbright Securities Co. Ltd, and China Everbright & Sun Life Insurance Company, and CEG is also the largest shareholder of Shenyin Wanguo Securities in mainland China. In addition, CEG owns three publicly listed companies in Hong Kong: China Everbright Ltd, China Everbright Technology Co. Ltd and China Everbright International Co. Ltd. By directly managing 60 enterprises by way of owning total shares, controlling majority of the shares, or participating as minor shareholder, CEG is making best use of markets resources both in mainland and Hong Kong to explore business potential.

2. The second largest shareholder of the Bank - China Everbright Limited (formerly known as Minghui Development Co. Ltd), went public in Hong Kong Stock Exchange in 1973. After years of business development, business re-adjustment and asset restructuring, China Everbright Limited has grown into a financial holdings company. Nowadays, China Everbright Limited is focusing on financial business, which includes banking, securities, futures trading, foreign exchange brokerage and insurance business.

#### OWNERSHIP RELATION AMONG THE TOP TEN SHAREHOLDERS

CEG is holding 55.47% of the shares of China Everbright Limited through China Everbright Holdings (HK) Ltd. Although Yuxi Hongta Tobacco (Group) Co. Ltd is directly responsible for China Tobacco Corporation Yunnan Province Branch, there is no equity relationship existing between them.

PROFILE OF COLLATERALIZED, ENTRUSTED AND FROZEN SHARES HELD BY TOP TEN SHAREHOLDERS

Among the top ten shareholders, CEG and China Ship Owners Mutual Assurance Association have respectively pledged all the Bank's shares they hold to certain enterprises and banks.

##### MAJOR RELATED-PARTY TRANSACTIONS

##### Loans

As at 31 December 2003, there were fifteen loan transactions to CEG, with the balance of loan at RMB2.164 billion yuan, for which CEG's share ownership was pledged, and classified as sub-standard according to five-category loan classification.

As at 31 December 2003, there were four loan transactions to Qingdao Guoxin Industrial Company, with the balance of loan at RMB311 million yuan, of which RMB116 million yuan was pledged by certificates of deposit, the rest was credit loan, and classified as normal by five-category loan classification.

As at 31 December 2003, there were two loan transactions to General Tobacco Group Co. Ltd, with the balance of loan at RMB100 million yuan, which was guaranteed by China Tobacco Corporation, Shandong Province Branch, and classified as normal by five-category loan classification.

There was one loan to Shanxi Haixin Steel Group Co. Ltd with the balance of RMB70 million yuan, for which share ownership was pledged, and classified as normal by five-category loan classification.

There was one loan to Shanghai Everbright Conference Center Co. Ltd with the balance of loan at RMB380 million yuan, for which land pledge was set, and classified as special mention by five-category loan classification.

There was one loan to Shanghai Everbright Travel and Property Company with the balance of RMB3 million yuan, which was guaranteed by China Everbright Travel Corporation, and defined as loss by five-category loan classification.

There was one loan to Guangzhou Shengye Real Estate Development Co. Ltd with the balance at RMB3.8 million yuan, which was guaranteed by CEG, and classified as doubtful by five-category loan classification.

There was one loan to Shunde Shuntong Group Co. Ltd with the balance at RMB5 million yuan, for which share ownership was pledged, and classified as doubtful by five-category loan classification.

There was one loan to Everbright Timber (Shenzhen) Co. Ltd with the balance at RMB34.50 million yuan, for which factory houses and land were pledged, and classified as special mention by five-category loan classification.

The total balance of the above-mentioned related-party transactions was RMB3.071 billion yuan, which accounted for 1.2% of the Bank's total loans, of which NPLs were RMB2.176 billion yuan, taking up 9.3%

the Bank's total NPLs and 27.49% of the total related-party transactions.

## Inter-financial institution lending

As at 31 December 2003, there were three lending transactions to China Everbright Securities Co. Ltd, with balance at RMB816 million yuan, of which RMB316 million yuan was overdue, RMB500 million yuan was normal.

As at 31 December 2003, there were three lending transactions to China Everbright Financial Co. Ltd, with balance at USD151.5 million, at HKD162 million, and falling into normal classification.

The balance of the above-mentioned transactions totaled RMB2.242 billion yuan, which accounted for 47.91% of the Bank's total inter-financial institution lending.

### Due from other financial institutions

CEB placed with China Everbright Securities Co. Ltd two deposits of RMB76.52 million yuan, which has already past due.

The total balance for above-mentioned deposits was RMB76.52 million yuan, which took up 0.84% of the Bank's total deposits from other banks.

### Guarantee

The Bank provided three guarantees to CEG with the balance at RMB2.4 billion yuan, which accounted for 26.9% of the Bank's total guarantee amount.

## LOANS TO SHAREHOLDERS THAT HAVE FALLEN INTO NON-PERFORMING STATUS

As at 31 December 2003, fifteen loans went to CEG, with the balance at RMB2.164 billion yuan, for all of which share ownership was pledged, and classified as sub-standard by five-category loan classification. The reason for the current situation is due to the historical problem left from before 2000. However, since they are pledged with share ownership, there should be no final risk.

As at 31 December 2003, two loans went to Beijing Zhongguancun Technology Development Holdings Co. Ltd, with the balance at RMB300 million yuan and classified as sub-standard by five-category loan classification, of which RMB100 million yuan was pledged by Beijing Zhongguancun Securities Co. Ltd using its share of ownership worth of RMB140 million yuan, of which RMB200 million yuan was guaranteed by Beijing Zhongguancun Development and Construction Co. Ltd., of which RMB112 million yuan was pledged by Beijing Zhongguancun Sihuan Medicine Development Co. Ltd on its stock share ownership. The reasons causing the current situation are accumulated liabilities, unexpected low return on the investment and poor liquidity.

As at 31 December 2003, two loans went to China Motorcycle Group Co. Ltd with the balance at RMB141.3 million yuan and classified as doubtful by five-category loan classification, of which RMB93 million yuan was guaranteed by Jinan Qingqi Motorcycle Co. Ltd, of which RMB 48.3 million yuan was guaranteed by Jinan Qingqi Suzuki Motorcycle Co. Ltd. The reason leading to the company's current situation is the historical problem left over from the former China Investment Bank, which was inefficient in operation, loose in management and losing market opportunities.

内部控制及风险管理状况评价报告

普华永道中天特审字(2004)第147号

中国光大银行股份有限公司全体股东：

我们接受委托，审计了中国光大银行股份有限公司（以下简称“贵行”）2003年度的会计报表。在审计过程中，我们了解和评价了贵行与会计报表编制有关的内部控制制度及风险管理机制，以确定我们实施会计报表审计之审计程序的性质、时间和范围，从而对贵行的会计报表发表审计意见。建立健全的内部控制制度和风险管理机制是贵行管理当局的责任。我们对贵行内部控制制度和风险管理机制的了解和评价是在《独立审计具体准则第9号－内部控制与审计风险》规定的控制目标基础上，结合我们的会计报表审计目的而进行的，而不是为发现内部控制制度和风险管理机制的缺陷或其他不规范行为而进行的。在对贵行内部控制制度和风险管理机制的了解和评价过程中，我们结合贵行的实际情况，实施了包括了解、观察及符合性测试等我们认为必要的程序。

根据我们的了解和评价,我们未发现贵行2003年度与会计报表编制有关的内部控制制度及风险管理机制与所附贵行《关于内控制度及风险管理的说明》有重大不符。

任何内部控制制度及风险管理机制均具有固有限制，存在由于错误或舞弊而导致错误发生和未被发现的可能性。此外，由于情况的变化可能导致内部控制制度及风险管理机制的不恰当，或对控制政策及程序遵循程度的降低，因此可以推测未来内部控制制度及风险管理机制在有效性方面存在一定的风险。

普华永道中天计师

# 关于内控制度及风险管理的说明

根据《中华人民共和国商业银行法》、《金融违法行为处罚办法》、《商业银行内部控制指引》等法律、行政法规要求，中国光大银行逐步形成了一套较为完整的内部控制和风险管理制度体系。这套制度体系较好地体现了内控机制和风险管理的基本要求，在强化一个法人体制，加强系统管理，依法合规经营，控制经营风险以及强化稽核监督等方面起到了积极的作用。

一、在完善决策规则，建立内控管理制衡系统方面，光大银行各级行在一个法人体制下建立了较为完备的决策系统、执行系统和监督反馈系统，并按照权力制衡的原则设置内部机构及部门。根据业务发展需要和银行经营与管理职能的规范化要求，2003年光大银行调整了信贷政策委员会、考评委员会、稽核监督委员会、资产负债管理委员会、新产品研究开发委员会、电子化建设委员会和大额支出集中采购审批委员会等议事机构及人员组成，进一步理顺了总行的管理和决策体制，提高了工作效率，并严格按照巴塞尔协议及银监会的监管要求，对全行的经营管理、资金运作及经营指标进行比例管理、风险评价、风险预警及风险控制。各分支行根据总行的要求，对部分职能机构进行了调整，多数行已经将原有的财务会计部分设为计划财务部和会计结算部，强化了各自不同的职能，增强了财务分析和财务管理的意识和能力，经营成本得到有效控制；加强了事后监督和会计督导工作，强化了资金清算与结算的分离和控制，会计核算进一步规范；各分支行对所辖机构派驻了柜台经理，加强了对操作风险的管理和控制。

二、在法人授权管理方面，光大银行按照中国人民银行《商业银行授权、授信管理暂行办法》制订了《中国光大银行授信业务授权管理办法》和《中国光大银行授信业务转授权管理办法》，严格进行授信授权管理。制订了同业融资专项授权操作指引，强化了对分支行同业往来的控制；明确了财务管理、人事管理授权，加强了对各分支行大额财务支出和重要部门负责人任免的管理工作。各级行基本能够坚持授权、转授权制度，认真履行职责，并对职责范围内的事项负责，对超授权事项按规定程序进行报批。同时，总行相关部门及内部稽核部门加强对授信授权、同业授权和财务授权等执行情况的监督检查，严惩越权绕权行为，一个法人管理体制得到了较好的落实。

三、在内部风险控制方面，构筑了较为有效的三道防线，使风险管理的措施能够得到落实。第一道防线是一线业务部门和相关核算部门，这些部门直接接触客户、资金和帐务，严格按照各项操作规程处理业务，岗位部门之间相互制约，最大限度地抵御各种外部和内部的风险。第二道防线是各管理部门，包括资金部、信贷审批部、信贷控制部、国际业务部、资产保全部等业务主管部门，其在内部控制中的任务是对一线操作部门业务开展情况进行检查、监控、统计、分析和评价，对职能范围内的风险部位、风险环节进行识别、审查、评价、监控，防范和化解各种隐患和风险，保障银行资产安全。第三道防线是内部稽核部门和监察保卫部门，在内部控制中承担评价内控体系和对业

务主管部门实施再监督职责，防范和化解由于工作失误、道德风险、制度缺陷等各种原因造成的风险。

四、在内控制度体系方面，正在随着业务的不断发展逐步完善。内控制度的内容涉及企业文化建设，机构岗位设置及职能界定，业务管理规章及操作规程、核算办法，决策、审批程序，新产品开发推广程序，业务风险评价，权限管理，印章管理，计算机系统风险控制，岗位任职、上岗资格及强制休假规定，安全保卫工作规定，机构、人员奖惩规定等，使业务操作及管理工作有章可循。监督、检查方面，除了有一套较为完整的稽核制度外，还制订了《中国光大银行内部控制综合评价办法》，从业务和管理的各个环节评价分支机构的内部控制状况；通过《中国光大银行信贷业务动态稽核管理办法》，适时对信贷资产的质量和管理状况进行检查和评价；《中国光大银行员工违反规章制度处理暂行办法》已成为执法检查、稽核、管理和处罚的基本依据，对贯彻落实各项规章制度、加强规范化管理具有重要促进作用。

五、在信贷风险管理方面，推动以风险控制为核心的信贷管理文化建设，多层次、多方位、多形式地在全行范围内大力倡导和灌输审慎的信贷风险管理理念，强化风险防范意识，努力建立良好的风险管理工作环境和氛围。贯彻落实贷款“三查”制度，按照统一授信制度及授信业务操作规程，加强信贷业务的过程控制和管理。注意开展地区、行业和产业研究，加强对企业经营状况的深入了解和评级，注重信贷风险分析，逐步调整信贷结构；严格执行放款审核岗制度，把住贷款的出口关；强调贷后检查工作，对授信实际用途和企业经营状况实施有效监控，完善风险预警机制，及时采取措施化解风险。以信贷风险管理项目建设为契机，推进信贷风险管理机制建设，在总行成立信贷审批委员会，将信贷政策的执行与信贷政策的制订和批准有效分离；将信贷管理部分设为信贷审批部和信贷控制部，分别强化了贷款审批和贷前贷后的审查与监控职能，并形成监督制约机制；成立了华东地区和南部地区两个审批中心，强化了总行对贷款的审查审批职能；在南部7家行试行了信贷管理体制改革，将试点行的信贷管理部改为信贷控制部，取消了其一般授信业务的审批权，进一步加强了信贷风险控制。以上措施保证了新增贷款的质量。

同时，光大银行继续加大不良资产清收保全力度，采取一切可能的措施，降低不良资产的潜在风险，努力提高资产质量，2003年实现了不良资产和不良资产率的双下降。

六、在内部监督机制方面，光大银行监事会、稽核、监察保卫三位一体共同构筑了本行的监督防范体系，并相互协调配合，加大监督力度。2003年银行监事会开展了对固定资产的专项审计；稽核部门完成了对全部30家分支行2002年各项经营指标的真实性检查，对部分分支行进行了全面稽核，实施了稽核结论和处理决定执行情况的后续稽核，各分支行稽核部也积极发挥职能作用，开展了对辖属机构的全面稽核、专项稽核、内控评价等工作。坚持每月对辖内业务状况进行非现场稽核分析评价，对稽核检查中发现的问题提出了限期整改要求。严格执行重要岗位责任人任期（离任）经

济责任稽核制度，对被稽核人任职期间的经营业绩和履行职责的情况进行客观评价，实事求是地指出存在的问题。对系统内发生的经济犯罪案件进行了深入的调查和分析，有针对性地提出了防范措施，进一步堵塞漏洞，强化内部管理，坚持依法合规经营。

七、进一步强化内控机制和风险管理的手段和措施。光大银行在近几年的工作中，对内控机制建设和风险管理工作给予了高度重视，并取得了较大进步。管理层也认识到，内部控制和风险管理是银行的一个永恒课题，是保持银行持续发展的重要基础。银行还将在未来的工作中进一步加强各级员工的风险意识，开展风险控制培训和警示教育，使各岗位人员从思想上明确风险控制的必要性和深远意义，增强执行规章制度的自觉性。加强对高级管理人员管理权力的有效约束，继续按照重要岗位责任人离任（任期）经济责任稽核制度的要求，加大对履职情况的监督检查。持续开展业务流程再造，并通过信息系统建设，规范各项业务操作，减少人的主观因素对业务处理过程的影响。在各分支行试行稽核部负责人派驻制，增强稽核部门的独立性和权威性，强化内部稽核职能，进一步发挥内部稽核部门对各岗位、各部门、各项业务实施再监督的作用。

## 集团客户授信业务的风险管理情况

本行制订了《中国光大银行集团客户授信业务风险管理暂行办法》。该办法所称“集团客户”是指符合以下特征之一的本行企事业法人授信对象（包括金融同业）：（1）在股权上或者经营决策上直接或间接控制其他企事业法人或被其他企事业法人控制的；（2）共同被第三方企事业法人所控制的；（3）主要投资者个人、关键管理人员或与其关系密切的家庭成员（包括三代以内直系亲属关系和二代以内旁系亲属关系）共同直接控制或间接控制的；（4）存在其他关联关系，可能不按公允价格原则转移资产和利润，本行认为应视同集团客户进行授信管理的。

集团客户信贷风险主要存在于对集团客户多头授信、过度授信和不适当分配授信额度，或集团客户经营不善，以及通过关联交易、资产重组等手段在内部关联方之间不按公允价格原则转移资产或利润等情况，导致不能按时收回贷款本金及利息。

为最大限度地防范信贷风险，本行对集团客户授信遵循以下原则：（1）统一原则。对集团客户

授信实行统一管理，即明示各成员公司的授信状况，集中管理和控制信贷风险。（2）适度原则。根据授信对象风险大小和自身风险承担能力，合理确定对集团客户的总体授信额度，防止过度集中风险。（3）预警原则。对集团客户建立风险预警机制，揭示其交叉违约情况，及时防范和化解集团客户授信风险。

### 不良资产管理的主要政策、措施及其效果

不良资产管理政策主要是以大力压缩不良资产存量为核心，坚持现金清收为主，债务重组、呆帐核销、以物抵债、诉讼追索、风险代理、公开拍卖等多种手段并举，将不良资产现金清收任务、抵债资产变现作为考核重点，加大对已核销呆帐的继续追索、抵债资产的盘活变现、世行转贷款项目的代理清收以及呆帐核销工作的管理力度。在不良资产的处置过程中体现“四个原则”：以直接收回现金为核心，化解风险；以符合条件的债务重组为形式，转化风险；以直接拍卖资产、变现为手段，降低风险；以公开、透明，集体决策的程序为原则处置抵债资产，控制风险。

不良资产管理措施主要是强调分类管理、分类指导，“一行一策、一户一策”；加大财务资源对降低不良资产清收的激励和支持力度；强调加大现金回收的比例，并给予一定的现金清收专项奖励。

由于政策与措施到位，不良资产清收取得明显效果，2003年共现金清收“两呆”类不良资产30.86亿元，其中清收本金18.08亿元，表内息1.75亿元，表外息1.8亿元，抵债资产处置收回

现金 5.62 亿元，租金收入 966 万元，现金收回世行转贷款 3.51 亿元。全年核销呆帐本金 24.31 亿元。截止年末，按四级分类口径，本行不良贷款余额 186.37 亿，比年初下降 51.6 亿元；不良贷款率 7.5%，比年初下降 4.94 个百分点；按五级分类口径，本行不良贷款余额 232.1 亿，比年初下降 19 亿元；不良贷款率 9.34%，比年初下降 3.79 个百分点。本行的不良贷款无论是按四级还是五级分类结果，实现了自 2002 年以来不良贷款率和不良贷款余额连续两年的“双降”。

### 表外业务风险管理情况

本行将表外业务纳入统一授信管理。比如，银行承兑汇票作为表外业务，本行在统一授信管理办法中，规定该产品的调节系数为1.2。在审批过程中，如果涉及到综合授信额度，本行采取“一揽子”批准的方式，包括表外产品和表内产品，提供授信金额和授信风险总量，明确品种之间是否可以串用。同时，高度重视表外业务的操作程序，规定办理承兑汇票业务时，客户必须要有购销合同复印件，增值税发票复印件等；办理融资保函业务，必须报总行审批。从2004年开始，本行在全系统实施表外业务的五级分类制度，这将进一步强化对表外业务的风险管理。

INTERNAL CONTROL AND RISK MANAGEMENT

SYSTEMS EVALUATION REPORT

12 $ ^{th} $ floor, Shui On Plaza

333 Huai Hai Zhong Lu

Shanghai 200021

Telephone +86 (21) 6386 3388
