#### To the Shareholders of China Everbright Bank Co., Ltd.,

We have been appointed to audit the financial statements of China Everbright Bank Co., Ltd. (the "Bank") for the yer ended 31 December 2003. During our audit, we understood and evaluated the internal control and risk management systems of the Bank related to the preparation of the financial statements to determine the nature, timing and scope of our audit procedures for expressing an audit opinion on the Bank's financial statements. The establishment of sound internal control and risk management systems is the responsibility of the Bank's management. Our understanding and evaluation of the Bank's internal control and risk management systems are based on the control objectives stipulated in the "Specific Independent Auditing Standard No. 9 - Internal Control and Audit Risks" and are conducted for the purpose of auditing the Bank's financial statements. They are not intended to identify the weaknesses or other irregularities in the internal control and risk management systems of the Bank. During our understanding and evaluation of the internal control and risk management systems of the Bank, considering the actual situations of the Bank, we have carried out such necessary procedures as understanding, observation and compliance tests.

Based on our understanding and evaluation, we have not found any material non-compliant instances in the Bank’s internal control and risk management systems during the preparation of the 2003 financial statements with the attached Explanation on the Internal Control and Risk Management Systems of the Bank.

Due to inherent limitations in any internal control and risk management systems, errors or irregularities may nevertheless occur as a result of mistakes and fraudulent activities and not be detected. Furthermore, projection of any evaluation of the systems to future periods is subject to the risk that management information and control procedures may become inadequate as a result of changes in the conditions or deterioration in the degree of compliance with those procedures.

PricewaterhouseCoopers Zhong Tian

CPAs Limited Company

##### ILLUSTRATIONS ON INTERNAL CONTROL AND RISK MANAGEMENT

In accordance with the Law of the People's Republic of China on Commercial Banks, Rules of Sanction on Offences Against Financial Laws and Regulations, Instruction on Internal Control for Commercial Banks and other applicable laws and regulations, China Everbright Bank (the Bank) has been devoted itself to formulating a set of relatively comprehensive system in internal control and risk management. So far, based on some essential requirements set out in the above system, the Bank's capability in internal control and risk management has been greatly upgraded through stressing sole-legal-person mechanism, strengthening systematic control and compliant business operation, effectively managing operational risk, and reinforcing internal audit and supervision over banking operations, which are expatiated as below.

## Improving Decision-making Process and Enhancing Check-and-balance Mechanism in Internal Control System

Under its uniform governance structure defined as centralized Sole Legal Person, the Bank has built up its sound decision-making system, effective implementation system, and interactive supervising and feedback system at all levels. In the mean time, the Bank structures its organizations and departments internally by following the Check-and-balance Principle.

To meet the need of rapid business growth, and as well as requirement by the standardized banking operation and management, the Bank took the following approaches to improve its operation and management at the Head-Office level: First, adjusted the composition of the below committees which are in charge of assessment and approval of respective business sectors: Credit Policy Committee, Performance Appraisal Committee, Auditing and Supervising Committee, Asset-Liability Management Committee, New Products Research and Development Committee, Computerization Committee, and Large Expenditure and Purchase Approval Committee. Second, further fine-tuned the management and decision-making mechanism of the Head Office to ensure more efficiency. Third, exactly adhering to the principles of Basel Accord and the regulatory requirements by China Banking Regulatory Commission, proportion control and all-round risk management, including risk pre-assessment, risk forecast and following-up risk control, were carried out throughout the bank over treasury operation, performance target setting and all other banking operations.

Accordingly, some other measures were also taken to enhance internal control at branch (sub-branch) level. First, some functional departments at the branch (sub-branch) level were restructured pursuant to the guidance of the Head Office. For example, the former Finance & Accounting Department in most branches were separated into two new departments, i.e. Planning & Finance Department, and Accounting & Settlement Department, so as to reinforce the specific functions of those newly established departments in respective areas. In so doing, the capacity of financial analysis and financial management at branch level has been strengthened and thus had the operating cost well under control. Second, following-up supervision and real-time direction for accounting affairs was further intensified. Third, separation of fund clearing from settlement was finally realized and the financial accounting was further standardized. Fourth, counter managers were designated from the branches (sub-branches) to corresponding banking outlets to ensure sound management and strict control of operational risk.

#### Strengthening Management in Legal Person's Delegation

In accordance with the Provisional Regulation on Delegation Management and Credit Approval Management promulgated by People's Bank of China, the Bank has worked out its Rules and Policies on Management of Delegation for Credit Approval and Rules and Policies on Management of Sub-delegation for Credit Approval

to ensure stringent management on credit approval and due delegation in this regard. Specifically, the Bank has formulated its operational manual on designated delegation for inter-placement with other financial institutions aiming at firmer control over the branches (sub-branches) on their inter-placement operation with other financial institutions. In addition, the Bank also actualized delegation on financial management and human resources management to enhance its rigorous control over large-amount expenditure by branches (sub-branches) on the one hand, and stressing its management on the recruitment and dismissal of chiefs for major departments at the branch (sub-branch) level on the other hand. As a result, job responsibilities are fully fulfilled at all levels, and all business proceedings exceeding due delegation were reported to higher-level authorities for approval in accordance with rules for delegation management and sub-delegation management. In the meanwhile, to well assure the implementation of Sole-Legal-Person System, Internal Auditing Department and other relative departments in Head Office also exerted supervision and inspection on offences against delegation management pursuant to delegation for credit approval, delegation for inter-placement with other financial institutions, and delegation for financial management.

### Highlighting Internal Risk Control Through Adequate Internal Functional Setting

A framework constituted by Three Firewalls has been built up to ensure all measures in risk management be fully put into effect. The front-line department and other departments relative to accounting affairs, which have face-to-face contact with the clients, get involved with funds transfer, or conduct record-keeping, are playing a key role interpreted as the Front Firewall. Based on the compliant operations of the above-mentioned departments and efficient check-and-balance among different departments and different posts, all kinds of risk, both internal and external, can be managed at the initial stage of business handling. The managerial or functional departments, such as Treasury Department, Credit Approval Department, Credit Control Department, International Business Department, and NPL Recovery Department, are acting as the Second Firewall. Those departments acting as the Second Firewall also play a significant role in internal control by preventing and mitigating explicit and hidden risks from all dimensions, and thus securing the safety of the bank's assets. The major functions of those departments compose the Middle Firewall are defined as below: to process inspection, carry out statistics, and make assessment accordingly over the performance of those operational departments on the front-line level; to identify the weak links or fields for risk-prevention in all managerial functions, and thereafter to follow up with further examination, evaluation and supervision correspondingly. The third Firewall is formed by Internal Auditing Department and Inspection & Security Department, which take great responsibility in internal control as appraising the internal control system and implementing overall supervision on the managerial and functional departments. Risks resulted from misconduct, poor business ethics, and system deficiencies, are hence well prevented or mitigated.

#### Formulating A Whole Set of Internal Control System

With continuous business development, the Bank has mapped out its comprehensive internal control policies, on which the bank's business operation and management are based, with a wide coverage on the following aspects: Corporate culture; Job description; Policies for all business lines, Operational manuals, and Accounting standard; Decision-making and approval procedures; New product development and promotion processing; Business risk assessment; Jurisdiction management; Stamp and Chop management; IT system risk control; Job qualification, Staff qualification appraisal and compulsory on-leave policy; Security rules; Reward and sanction rules. As far as business supervision and inspection are concerned, in addition to a whole set of auditing policies, the Bank also established the following rules and policies: Rules On Consolidated Evaluation of Internal Control For China Everbright Bank, which focuses on assessing the

internal control performance by investigating into the branches' operation and management activities; Rules On On-going Auditing Over Provided Credit Facilities for China Everbright Bank, which is frequently referred to in inspecting and appraising the quality of the credit assets, and evaluating the performance in managing those assets as well; Provisional Regulation On Sanctions to Offences Against Rules and Policies By Staff Members For China Everbright Bank, on which investigation, auditing and sanctions are mainly based to ensure all rules and policies of the bank be fully implemented and executed, and its management in standardization well achieved.

#### Focusing on Credit Risk Management

Favorable atmosphere and circumstance for sound credit risk management has been built up within the bank through efforts as follows: Devoting in cultivation of credit management culture with key focus on risk control; Advocating widely the philosophy of credit risk management as Due Prudence and Reasonable Caution; Bettering awareness of the significance of risk-prevention. In daily credit risk management practice, procedure control was well performed, while loan inspection was duly undertaken in accordance with uniform credit approval policies and credit-facilities operational rules; Special attention was paid to research on different regions and industries in relation to credit risk management. In-depth pre-rating investigation was made to get better knowledge on the operation of enterprises concerned, and detailed rating analysis emphasizing credit risk diagnosis was followed afterwards helping achieve an optimized portfolio of credit assets; Strict control was observed in loan granting process through the operation of Loan Release Centers; Follow-up inspection on finance projects and investigation into relative enterprises were added up to the whole procedure of risk-control in way of effective risk pre-warning. With the introduction of the Credit Risk Management project, credit risk management restructuring was under way. The establishment of Credit Approval Committee realized effective separation between the function of credit approval and credit policy implementation from that of credit policy enactment.; The former Credit Management Department was separated into two cross-supervising new departments, i.e., Credit Approval Department and Credit Control Department, which are respectively in charge of loan approval, and pre-inspection as well as following-up control; Two Sub-centers set up to facilitate loan approval processing in South-east and Southern regions of China, further improved the Bank's capability in loan appraisal; Throughout seven branches where a pilot reform of credit management was launched, the former Credit Management Departments were now restructured into Credit Control Departments, with all of their authority in loan granting being deprived and centralized for the reason of better credit risk control. All the above approaches taken helped improve the overall quality of loans newly released.

To lower down the potential risks resulting from existing NPLs and improve the quality of its assets to the utmost, the Bank furthered its efforts in NPL recovery through all sorts of means. As a result, both the outstanding balance of NPLs and the NPL ratio of the bank were decreased in the year 2003.

#### Bettering Internal Supervisory Mechanism

The Bank has developed its supervisory system under the joint efforts by the Supervisory Board, Auditing force, and Inspection & Security force. With close coordination and cooperation among the above three forces, the supervision over the bank's the operation and management was further enhanced. In the year 2003, the Bank's Supervisory Board audited some specific fixed asset projects. The Auditing Department conducted extensive auditing on authenticity and genuineness of performance results throughout all of the 30 branches (sub-branches). It also carried out all-round auditing over some branches (sub-branches), and came up with follow-up auditing on the implementation of improvement requirements based on former

auditing results. The auditing departments at branch level also play active role in auditing on overall banking operation, auditing on specific business proceedings, and general evaluation on internal control. Off-site auditing and supervision over banking operation were made on a monthly basis, with all problems audited out being required to correct within a limited period of time. For those chief personnel leaving their posts, strict auditing on individual performance and fulfillment of obligations was conducted before their resignation to reach an objective conclusion, and all problems found are made clear based on facts. Criminal offences were fully investigated with the purpose of coming up with future prevention measures and assure future operational compliance.

#### Commitment in Future Internal Control and Risk Management

In last couple of years, the Bank has achieved considerable accomplishment by attaching great importance in internal control and risk management. The top management has reached the consensus that, internal control and risk management is essential for the bank's future development and thus continuous devotion in this regard is required. The Bank is committed to highlighting the awareness of risk management for its staff members at all levels in future, by holding training programs and seminars in risk control arena to fully display the significance of risk control. The Bank would also dedicate its effort in supervision and control over the senior management team on their performance, adhering to relevant leaving-the-post or on-the-post auditing policies. All aspects of operations for current business lines will be standardized, and protected from interference as well, through further process restructuring and IT implementation. All the chiefs of the Auditing Departments at branch (sub-branch) level will be appointed from the Head Office, a reform to be launched as a pilot program in enforcing stronger internal auditing so as to maintain the independence and prevailing authority of the auditing departments, and give full play to internal auditing department in its supervision over individual performance, all other departments and all business lines.

##### Risk management of credit facilities to group customers

The Bank has formulated Provisional Rule on Risk Management of Credit Facilities Allocation for Group customers (the Rule). The so-called group customers in the Rule mean those enterprises and institutions (including financial institutions) which have any of the following features: (1) controlling or controlled by other enterprises or institutions in terms of ownership or decision making; (2) jointly controlled by a third enterprise or institutions; (3) jointly controlled, directly or indirectly, by the same major investors, same major managers, or by people of close kinship relations (including directly-related family members within three generations and collateral relatives within two generations); (4) having other relations that may result in assets and profits transfer not at fair market value and should be regarded as group customers by the Bank.

The risks in the group customers mainly lies in multiple-entity credit allocation, excessive credit allocation, inappropriate distribution of credit lines, or poor management and ill-manner transfer of assets and profits not at arms-length among group parties by means of related party transactions, asset restructuring, etc. that may result in unduly repayment of principals and interests.

The Bank follows the below principles in allocating credit lines to group customers in order to mitigate credit risk to the utmost extent. (1) Unification principle. Practice unified management on credit allocation to group customers, namely disclosing the credit lines for each member company, and centralize management and control of credit lines. (2) Principle of modest allocation of credit lines. Appropriately fix a general credit line to the target group customer in accordance with its risk profile and the Banks own mitigation capability in order to prevent excessive concentration of risks. (3) Precaution principle. Establish an early warning system for group customers through disclosing its cross-default, timely mitigate credit risks of group customers.

##### Investments

#### Main policies, measures and effectiveness of management on non-performing assets

The goals of non-performing asset management policies are to focus on reducing the amount of existing non-performing assets, adhere to cash recollection as the main measure, adopting multiple methods such as debt restructuring, bad debt writing-offs, offsetting debts with collaterals, debt recollection via legal proceedings, public auctions, etc, and establishing a rational compensation system for bad asset workout focused on cash recollection of both non-performing assets and settled assets. The Bank also strengthened management of ongoing collection of written-off debts, liquidization of settled assets, resurrection of World Bank sub-loans and write-off of bad debts. Four basic principles are reflected in the process of non-performing asset disposal, namely mitigating risks with cash collection as the focus, shifting off risks by adhering to qualified and rational debt restructuring, reducing risks by means of selling assets, and controlling risks by adhering to the principles of openness, transparency and collective decision-making process for disposal of settled assets.

The measures on managing non-performing assets emphasize on differential management and guidance for each branch and each customer. The bank also mobilizes more financial resources to support and stimulate bad asset workout efforts; and tries to increase the proportion of cash collection by setting up special reward for cash collection.

Due to proper implementation of relevant policies and measures, tangible results have been achieved in non-performing asset collection. In 2003, a total of RMB3.086 billion yuan non-performing assets were recollected in cash, out of which recovered principals totaled to RMB1.808 billion yuan, in-balance-sheet interest receivables totaling to RMB175 million yuan, off-balance-sheet interest receivables totaling to RMB180 million yuan, cash collection by disposing of settled assets totaling to RMB562 million yuan, rental income totaling to RMB9.66 million yuan, cash collection of World Bank sub-loans totaling to RMB351 million yuan. A total of RMB2.431 billion yuan bad debts were written off in the year. By the end of 2003, according to the four-category-classification standard, total non-performing loans of the Bank amounted to RMB18.637 billion yuan, decreasing by RMB5.16 billion yuan compared with that of the year beginning; while NPL ratio stood at 7.5%, down 4.94 percentage points as compared with that of the year beginning. According to five-category-classification standard, the Bank's NPL balance amounted to RMB23.21 billion yuan, decreasing by RMB1.9 billion yuan as compared with that of the year beginning; while NPL ratio stood at 9.34%, down by 3.79 percentage points as compared with that of the year beginning. No matter

based on the four-category or five-category loan classification standards, the Bank's NPL ratio and NPL balance have been reduced concurrently for two consecutive years since 2002.

## 2. Risk management of off-balance-sheet businesses

The Bank integrates off-balance sheet businesses into the spectrum of a unified credit management. For instance, arisk weight of 1.2 is affixed to bank acceptance draft business according to the Bank's Rules on Unified Credit Allocation Management. During the examination and approval process, so far as a multi-product credit line allocation is required, the Bank shall approve a package credit line for bother in-balance-sheet and off-balance-sheet items, clearly defining total amount and risk exposure of the credit line, and clarifying whether a different product lines thereunder can be shared with each other. Meantime, close attention is paid to the operating procedures of off-balance-sheet business. For instance, in discounting process of accepted drafts, clients are requested to present copies of sales contract, copies of invoices of added value tax, etc. Each financing guarantee transaction must be submitted to Head Office for approval. From year 2004 on, the Bank will implement five-category-classification standard on the off-balance-sheet businesses, which will further strengthen risk management of off-balance-sheet businesses.

### 报告期内重要诉讼、仲裁事项和重大案件情况

本年度，就本行所接收的原中国投资银行与中国建设银行的拆借纠纷事宜，在有关部门的协调下，本行与中国建设银行达成和解协议。和解方案如下：

1、对于中国建设银行总行和光大银行总行之间的1.99亿美元的外汇拆借资金，中国建设银行不再向光大银行追偿。

2、对于中国建设银行分支机构和光大银行分支机构之间的拆借资金，由光大银行向中国建设银行偿还本金共计人民币8.6亿元，分三年还清，利息全部免除。

经有关部门批准，我行将豁免债务22.88亿元人民币与相对应的不良资产剥离表外核算，2003年底完成账务处理。

报告期内无重要仲裁和重大案件事项。

### 报告期内的收购、合并及出售资产事项

报告期内发生信贷资产出售业务4笔，涉及金额25亿元。

报告期内无收购、合并事项。

## 报告期内重大的托管、担保、承诺、委托资产管理情况

1、取得代理开放式基金业务资格：2003年1月，中国人民银行、中国证券监督管理委员会以《中国人民银行、中国证券监督管理委员会关于中国光大银行代理开放式基金业务的批复》（银复[2003]20号），批准光大银行代理开放式基金业务。

2、取得QFII托管人资格：2003年2月，《中国人民银行关于中国光大银行开办合格境外机构投资者境内证券投资托管业务的批复》（银复[2003]28号），批准光大银行开办合格境外机构投资者境内证券投资（QFII）托管业务；2003年9月，中国证券监督管理委员会《关于中国光大银行合格境外机构投资者托管人资格的批复》（证监基金字[2003]103号），批准光大银行QFII托管人资格。

3、成功托管一只开放式基金——“中融融华债券型证券投资基金”：该基金2003年4月16日设立，2003年年末基金资产净值112789万元，基金份额106483万份，当年实现托管费收入282万元。

4、托管一只受托资产托管产品——“光大平衡”资产托管业务：2003年7月29日正式签约，该产品资产总规模2亿元。

5、与上海市企业年金发展中心达成委托资产托管业务合作协议。

担保、承诺为本行正常业务，报告期内无重大事项。

报告期内没有新发生委托资产管理业务。

报告期内本行及其董事、高级管理人员未受到监管部门和司法部门的任何处罚

##### SIGNIFICANT LITIGATION, ARBITRATION AND OTHER LAWSUITS DURING THE REPORTING PERIOD

During year 2003, after coordination efforts made by relevant government bodies, the Bank reached reconciliation agreement with China Construction Bank on the inter-bank placement dispute between the former China Investment Bank, which has been acquired by the Bank, and China Construction Bank. The reconciliation agreement is as follows:

1. For the foreign exchange borrowing of USD199 million between the head offices of the two banks, China Construction Bank would no longer claim repayment from the Bank.

2. For the placements between the branches of the two banks, the Bank would repay RMB860 million yuan during three-year period with interests thereof exempted.

Upon approval by relevant government bodies, the Bank put the above exempted debt and the corresponding non-performing assets into off-balance sheet items. The relevant financial settlement was completed by the end of year 2003.

There were no significant arbitration and litigations, during the reporting period.

#### MERGER, ACQUISITION AND SELLING OF ASSETS DURING THE REPORTING PERIOD

No mergers or acquisitions occurred during the reporting period.

There were four transactions of selling out credit assets involving RMB2.5 billion yuan during the reporting period.

## MAJOR CUSTODY, GUARANTEE, COMMITMENT, AND ENTRUSTED ASSET MANAGEMENT IN THE REPORTING PERIOD

1. Obtain qualification for conducting open-end fund custody business:

Pursuant to the Reply of PBOC and China Securities Regulatory Commission for the Bank to Conduct Open-end Fund Custody Business (Yinfu[2003]20) issued in January 2003, the Bank was approved to provide open-end fund custody service.

## 2. Obtain qualification for acting as QFII custodian.

According to the Reply of the PBOC for the Bank to Act as Custodian for QFII Making Investment in Domestic Securities (Yinfu[2003]28), the Bank was approved to conduct the business. In September 2003 granted the Bank with qualification as custodian for QFII with the Reply on CEB Qualification for Acting as Custodian for QFII (Zhenjianjijinzi[2003]103).

3. Successfully distributed as agent for an open-end fund - Zhongrong Ronghua Securities Bond Fund. The fund was established on April 16, 2003. By the end of the year, the net asset value of the fund was RMB1,127,890,000 yuan with 1,064,830,000 units. The custody income realized for the year was

RMB2,820,000 yuan.

4. Managed an entrusted assets custody product- "Everbright Balance" asset management business. The custody agreement was signed on 29 July 2003. The whole size of the assets of this product was RMB200 million yuan.

5. Entered into agreement with Shanghai Enterprises Annuity Development Center on asset management. Both guarantee and commitment are the Bank's normal business, and there was no important events occurred during the reporting period.

No new entrusted asset management business occurred during the reporting period.

我们审计了后附的中国光大银行股份有限公司(以下简称“贵行”)2003年12月31日的资产负债表以及2003年度的利润表和现金流量表。这些载于第86页至第117页的会计报表的编制是贵行管理当局的责任，我们的责任是在实施审计工作的基础上对这些会计报表发表意见。

我们按照中国注册会计师独立审计准则计划和实施审计工作，以合理确信会计报表是否不存在重大错报。审计工作包括在抽查的基础上检查支持会计报表金额和披露的证据，评价管理当局在编制会计报表时采用的会计政策和作出的重大会计估计，以及评价会计报表的整体反映。我们相信，我们的审计工作为发表意见提供了合理的基础。

我们认为，上述会计报表符合中华人民共和国国家颁布的企业会计准则和中华人民共和国财政部于1993年颁布的《金融企业会计制度》及在会计报表附注中涉及的相关银行会计的规定，在所有重大方面公允地反映了贵行2003年12月31日的财务状况以及2003年度的经营成果和现金流量。

中国光大银行股份有限公司 会计报表附注

截至 2003 年 12 月 31 日止年度

(除特别注明外，金额单位为人民币千元)

## 一 银行简介

中国光大银行股份有限公司（“本行”）于1992年8月18日于中华人民共和国（“中国”）北京市成立，成立时为中国光大（集团）总公司（“光大集团”）全资拥有的金融企业，注册资本为15亿元，实收资本为7.75亿元。

根据中国人民银行银复[1995]70号《关于中国光大银行改制的批复》以及中国人民银行银管[1997]8号《关于核准中国光大银行股东资格的批复》，本行于1997年1月15日完成了增资改制，正式成为一家股份制商业银行，注册资本及实收资本增加至28亿元。经重组及增发新股后，本行总发行股本合计28亿股。于1997年8月，光大集团将其持有的本行股份5.6亿股转让予于香港联合交易所有限公司上市的中国光大控股有限公司（“光大控股”）。

于1999年经股东大会通过并经中国人民银行银复[1999]278号文批准，本行将注册资本由28亿元增加到43.12亿元。增资资金15.12亿元由资本公积转拨。每股面值1元的15.12亿股新股按原股东的持股比例转增，每100股转增54股。新增资本已经由安达信·华强会计师事务所验证，并于2000年9月22日出具验资报告。

于2000年经股东大会通过并经中国人民银行银复[2000]217号文批准，于2000年10月17日本行以每股1.95元的价格增发每股面值为1元的18.42亿股普通股。新发行股份按原股东持股比例向原股东配售，以现金方式认购。原股东放弃认购的配售股部分，由新投资者认购。鉴于募股资金充裕，本行向中国人民银行申请调增募股总量。根据中国人民银行银复[2001]147号《中国人民银行关于中国光大银行调增募股总量的批复》，本行的注册资本由43.12亿元变更为74.699亿元，新增资本已经由普华永道中天会计师事务所有限公司验证，并于2003年6月2日出具验资报告。

于 2002 年 4 月 30 日经股东大会通过并经中国人民银行银复[2002]119 号文批准，本行注册资本由 74.699 亿元增加到 82.1689 亿元，增资余额 7.4699 亿元由资本公积转拨。每股面值 1 元的 7.4699 亿股新普通股按原股东的持股比例转增，每 10 股转增 1 股，股权登记工作已于 2002 年 5 月 30 日前完成。新增资本已经由普华永道中天会计师事务所有限公司验证，并于 2003 年 6 月 3 日出具验资报告。本次变更前后的股本结构为：

经中国人民银行批准，本行与国家开发银行（“开行”）于1999年3月18日签订了《国家开发银行与中国光大银行关于转让（接收）原中国投资银行债权债务及同城营业网点的协议》（“转让协议”）。根据该转让协议，开行以转让协议签订日为基准日，将原中国投资银行（“原投行”）的资产、负债、所有者权益及原投行二十九个分支行的137家同城营业网点，转让给本行。转让协议自1999年3月18日起生效。本行根据转让协议，以原投行于协议生效日的账面值记录接收的资产负债。于2001年，经中华人民共和国财政部（“财政部”）批

准，本行对接收的原投行净资产进行重新核定，并将收购成本与净资产公允价值之间的差额65.37亿元确认为商誉（附注五、21）。

本行的经营范围包括的本外币业务有：吸收公众存款；发放短期、中期和长期本外币贷款；办理国内外结算；办理票据贴现；发行金融债券；代理发行、代理兑付、承销政府债券、买卖政府债券；从事同业拆借；买卖、代理买卖外汇；提供信用证服务及担保；代理收付款项及代理保险业务；提供保管箱业务及经中国人民银行和国家外汇管理局批准经营的其他金融业务。

## 二 重大会计政策变更

### 1 法定盈余公积金及公益金

本行于2003年度改变了提取法定盈余公积金及公益金的入帐时间，由在董事会批准期间入帐变更为在其所属期间入帐。本行对上述会计政策的变更进行了追溯调整，调减了2002年度期初未分配利润3,521万元，调增了2002年度盈余公积4,073.7万元，调减了2003年度期初未分配利润4,073.7万元（附注五、37）。

### 2 委托存贷款

本行于 2003 年度变更了委托存贷款的列示方法，由表内列示变更为表外列示。

## 三 会计报表编制基准

本会计报表按照中华人民共和国财政部颁布的企业会计准则及于1993年颁布实施的中华人民共和国《金融企业会计制度》及其他由财政部、中国人民银行、中国银行业监督管理委员会（以下简称“银监会”）及其他相关部门颁布的适用于本行进行会计核算和处理的相关规定及通知编制，相关的规定详见会计报表附注的各相关部分。

本会计报表附注的披露同时也遵照了中国人民银行2002年颁布的《商业银行信息披露暂行办法》。

本会计报表为汇总会计报表，以本行总行本部、各分行及直属支行的个别会计报表及其他资料为基础汇总编制。汇总时，本行各级机构之间的重大交易及其余额已相互抵销。

## 四 主要会计政策

### 1 会计期间

本行会计年度为公历1月1日起至12月31日止。

### 2 记账本位币

以人民币为记账本位币。本会计报表的编制金额单位为人民币千元。

### 3 记账基础和计价原则

除特别说明外，本行的记帐基础是权责发生制，所有报表项目均以历史成本计价。

### 4 外币业务核算方法及外币会计报表的折算方法

对于外币业务，采用分账制核算。

年终决算时，对各种分账核算的外币账户余额按照决算日中国人民银行公布的基准汇率将各种外币折算成人民币，与人民币决算会计报表汇总编制汇总人民币决算会计报表。资产负债表中的所有资产及负债项目及利润表项目均按资产负债表报告日中国人民银行公布的基准汇率或经国家认可的套算汇率折算，由此而产生的汇兑损益计入当期损益。股东权益项目除未分配利润项目外，均按业务发生当日中国人民银行公布的基准汇率折算，未分配利润项目以折算后利润表中该项目的累计金额确认；由于资产负债表报告日汇率与实际投入当日汇率不同而形成的差异计入折算后资产负债表的资本公积项目。

### 5 现金及现金等价物

现金是指库存现金及可随时用于支付的银行存款；现金等价物是指持有期限不超过90天、易于转换为已知金额且价值变动风险很小的资产，包括存放中央银行的备付金、存放同业活期款项和存期不超过90天的定期存款等货币性资产。

### 6 坏账核算方法

本行对各资产可能发生的坏帐损失采用备抵法核算。

提取的坏账准备计入当期损益。对于有确凿证据表明应收款项确实无法收回时，首先冲减已计提的坏账准备，不足部分直接计入当期损益。对于确认为坏账损失并已核销的应收款项如又收回的，在收回时，按原核销的坏账准备金额转回。

### 7 应收账款

应收账款为本行的应收利息。

本行根据财政部颁布的财金[2001]127号文《财政部关于印发〈金融企业呆账准备提取及呆账核销管理办法〉的通知》的规定（附注四、10），对除贷款应收利息外的其他应收利息按年末余额计提准备金。

### 8 贴现

贴现主要包括本行向持有已经承兑且尚未到期商业汇票的客户的贴现，还包括本行因资金管理需要而将客户贴入票据向中央银行进行的再贴现、向其他金融机构进行的转贴现，以及接受其他金融机构向本行的转贴现。

贴现业务以贴现票据的到期价值计量，贴现票据的到期价值与所支付的票据贴现款项之间的差额作为贴现利息，计入贴现当期损益。

本行根据财政部颁布的财金[2001]127号文《财政部关于印发〈金融企业呆账准备提取及呆账核销管理办法〉的通知》的规定（附注四、10），对贴现按年末余额计提准备金。

### 9 贷款分类方法

#### (a) 短期及中长期贷款

本行按贷款期限确定贷款类别，期限在1年以内（含1年）的贷款列作短期贷款，期限在1年以上的贷款列作中长期贷款。到期日在1年以内的中长期贷款均列示于短期贷款。

#### (b) 逾期贷款

借款人到期（含展期后到期）不能归还的贷款；或贴现汇票到期承兑人不能按期支付，且贴现申请人账户存款不足形成的被动垫款；或本行承兑的汇票到期，承兑申请人存款不足等原因形成的被动垫款；或因信用证及担保等表外业务项下的垫付款项，从垫付日起即列为逾期贷款。

##### (c) 呆滞贷款

逾期贷款逾期满90天后即转为呆滞贷款。

#### (d) 呆账贷款

呆账贷款的确认标准如下：

（1）借款人和担保人依法宣告破产、关闭、解散，并终止法人资格，本行对借款人和担保人进行追偿后，未能收回的债权；

(2) 借款人死亡，或者依照《中华人民共和国民法通则》的规定宣告失踪或死亡，本行依法对其财产或者遗产进行清偿，并对担保人进行追偿后，未能收回的债权；

(3) 借款人遭受重大自然灾害或者意外事故，损失巨大且不能获得保险赔偿，或者以保险赔偿后，确实无力偿还部分或者全部债务，以其财产进行清偿和对担保人进行追偿后，未能收回的债权；

（4）借款人和担保人虽未依法宣告破产、关闭、解散，但已完全停止经营活动，被县级及县级以上工商行政管理部门依法注销、吊销营业执照、终止法人资格，本行对借款人和担保人进行追偿后，未能收回的债权；

(5) 借款人触犯刑律，依法受到制裁，其资产不足归还所借债务，又无其他债务承担者，本行经追偿后确实无法收回的债权；

(6) 由于借款人和担保人不能偿还到期债务，本行诉诸法律，经法院对借款人和担保人强制执行，借款人和担保人均无财产可执行，法院裁定终结执行后，仍无法收回的债权；

（7）由于上述（1）至（6）项原因借款人不能偿还到期债务，本行依法取得的抵债资产的预计可收回金额，例如，经评估确认的市场公允价值，扣除抵债资产接收费用后，小于贷款本息的差额，经追偿后仍无法收回的债权；

(8) 开立信用证、办理承兑汇票、开具保函等发生垫款时，凡开证申请人和保证人由于上述(1)至(7)项原因，无法偿还垫款，经追偿后仍无法收回的垫款；

(9) 银行卡被伪造、冒用、骗领而发生的应由本行承担的净损失；

（10）助学贷款逾期后，本行在确定的有效追索期内，并依法处置助学贷款抵押物（质押物）和向担保人追索连带责任后，仍无法收回的贷款；

(11) 经国务院专案批准核准核销的债权。

#### 呆账准备核算方法

本行采用备抵法核算呆账准备。

本行根据财政部颁布的财金[2001]127号文《财政部关于印发〈金融企业呆账准备提取及呆账核销管理办法〉的通知》的规定，于2001年1月1日起，建立统一的呆账准备制度，不再提取坏账准备和投资风险准备，不再单独申报核销坏账损失和投资损失。截止2000年12月31日的呆账准备、坏账准备和投资风险准备余额一并转入统一的呆账准备账户管理。

本行根据提取呆账准备的资产的风险大小确定呆账准备的计提比例。呆账准备期末余额最高为提取呆账准备资产期末余额的100%，最低为提取呆账准备资产期末余额的1%。

同时，根据上述财金[2001]127号文的规定，对截至2000年12月31日以前发生的呆账，本行根据自身的经营情况自2001年起在不超过5年的时间内逐步提足呆账准备。本行将根据中国人民银行颁布的银发[2002]98号文《银行贷款损失准备计提指引》的规定，在2005年底以前按照贷款五级分类的状况计提贷款损失准备。

呆账准备的提取范围包括贷款，贴现，拆放款项，存放同业款项，进出口押汇，买入返售，除应收贷款利息外的其他应收利息，除国债外的其他长、短期投资，催收款项，应收租赁款及待处理抵债资产等。

## 11 短期投资

本行短期投资为自营买卖的国债及金融债券。短期投资以实际支付的全部价款扣除已到付息期但尚未领取的债券利息后的余额计价。短期投资持有期间所收到的利息除收到的已计入应收项目的利息外均直接冲减投资账面价值，在转让或到期兑付时确认投资损益。

本行根据财政部颁布的财金[2001]127号文《财政部关于印发〈金融企业呆账准备提取及呆账核销管理办法〉的通知》的规定（附注四、10），对除国债外的其他短期投资按年末余额计提准备金。

## 12 长期投资

长期投资包括长期债券投资、长期投资应计利息和其他长期投资。

长期债券投资取得时按实际成本计价，实际成本为实际支付的全部价款，扣除经纪人佣金、税金、手续费等附加费用，并扣除自债券发行日或上一次结息日起至购买日止期间内的应计利息后的余额，实际成本与债券票面价值的差额，作为长期债券投资的溢／（折）价在债券存续期间按直线法摊销。长期债券投资按期计提利息收入，对于在债券到期日一次性还本付息的债券投资，其尚未收到的利息列示于“长期投资应计利息”项目下；对于在债券存续期限内分期支付利息的，在资产负债表日将尚未收到的利息列示于“应收账款”项目下。

其他长期投资为本行对被投资单位无控制、无共同控制且无重大影响的股权及基金投资。长期股权投资的成本按投资时实际支付的价款入账。长期股权投资采用成本法核算，投资收益在被投资单位宣告分派利润或现金股利时予以确认。

本行根据财政部颁布的财金[2001]127号文《财政部关于印发〈金融企业呆账准备提取及呆账核销管理办法〉的通知》的规定（附注四、10），对除国债外的其他长期投资按年末余额计提准备金。

## 13 固定资产及折旧

固定资产是指为生产商品、提供劳务、出租或经营管理而持有的，使用年限超过1年且单位价值较高的房屋、建筑物、与生产经营有关的生产经营设备。

固定资产按购置或新建时的原始成本计价。固定资产折旧采用直线法计算并按原价减去估计3%的净残值后在估计经济使用年限内平均计提。

固定资产的类别、估计经济使用年限列示如下：


<table border=1 style='margin: auto; word-wrap: break-word;'><tr><td style='text-align: center; word-wrap: break-word;'>房屋及建筑物</td><td style='text-align: center; word-wrap: break-word;'>5－35年</td></tr><tr><td style='text-align: center; word-wrap: break-word;'>运输工具</td><td style='text-align: center; word-wrap: break-word;'>4－6年</td></tr><tr><td style='text-align: center; word-wrap: break-word;'>家具、固定装置及办公设备</td><td style='text-align: center; word-wrap: break-word;'>3－10年</td></tr></table>

当固定资产在实质上已经不能再给本行带来经济利益时，本行予以全额计提减值准备；当固定资产在实质上为本行创造经济利益的能力受到重大不利影响时，本行根据该项资产的账面价值超过其可收回金额的差异予以计提减值准备。

单项资产的可收回金额是指其销售净价与其使用价值两者之中的较高者。销售净价是指在熟悉交易情况的交易各方之间自愿进行的公平交易中，通过销售该项资产而取得的、扣除处置费用后的金额。使用价值指预期从资产的持续使用和使用寿命结束时的处置中形成的预计未来现金流量的现值。

当前期间导致该项资产发生减值的迹象可能已经全部或部分消失时，则依据重新计算的可收回金额并在以前年度已确认的减值损失范围内予以转回。

## 14 在建工程

在建工程指正在兴建中或安装中的资本性资产，以实际发生的支出作为工程成本入账。成本的计价包括建筑费用及其他直接费用、机器设备原价、安装费用，还包括在达到预定可使用状态之前为该项目专门借款所发生的借款费用。在建工程达到预定可使用状态时转入固定资产。

## 15 商誉

商誉是指购买成本超过本行应占所收购的可辨认资产和负债公允价值的部分。商誉作为资产确认，并以原值减累计摊销和累计减值损失列账，以直线法按20年的期限平均摊销。

## 16 长期待摊费用及摊销

长期待摊费用是指已经支出，但摊销期限在1年以上（不含1年）的各项费用。

本行长期待摊费用主要为经营用房的装修支出、租赁费以及其他资本性支出，按实际发生额入账。长期待摊费用还包括尚未开业的分支机构筹建期间的费用。

经营用房的装修支出以直线法按实际租赁期限或预计使用年限两者孰短的期限平均摊销。

对于尚未开业的分支机构在其筹建期间所发生的费用，先在长期待摊费用中归集，待其正式开始营业的当月一次计入营业费用。

## 17 待处理抵债资产

待处理抵债资产是贷款客户在无力以货币资金抵偿银行的债权时，根据双方约定或法院生效裁定，以其自有（或第三人）的动产或不动产折价抵偿债务。本行在确认抵债资产的所有权转移后，根据法院裁决确定的价值、借贷双方共同认可的权威评估部门评估的价值或借贷双方协议的价值与贷款本金和表内应收利息之和孰低入账。本行抵债资产的类别包括房产、土地、机器设备、车辆和股权等。

鉴于待处理抵债资产源于贷款的偿还并且其持有目的为变现偿债而非用于生产商品、提供劳务、出租或经营管理，因此本行并未将待处理抵债资产作固定资产管理及核算。本行根据财政部颁布的财金[2001]127号文《财政部关于印发〈金融企业呆账准备提取及呆账核销管理办法〉的通知》的规定（附注四、10），对待处理抵债资产按年末余额计提准备金，处置抵债资产时产生的损益计入当期损益。

## 18 收入确认原则

贷款利息收入（包括拆放同业及拆放金融性公司，下同）按照协议利率按期计算确认。

除在相关的主要会计政策特别说明外，其他收入按权责发生制原则确认。

根据财政部颁布的财金[2002]5号文件《关于缩短金融企业应收利息核算期限的通知》：

– 贷款利息自结息日起，逾期90天（含90天）以内的应收未收利息，应继续计入当期损益；贷款利息逾期90天（不含90天）以上，无论贷款本金是否逾期，发生的应收未收利息不再计入当期损益，在表外核算，实际收回时再计入损益。

－ 对已经纳入损益的应收未收利息，在其贷款本金或应收利息逾期超过90天（不含90天）以后，金融企业要相应作冲减利息收入处理。

同时，根据财政部颁发的财金字[2001]25号文件《财政部关于调整金融企业应收利息核算办法的通知》：

– 对于 2000 年 12 月 31 日以前纳入损益的应收未收利息，金融企业可以根据自身的经营情况，在不超过 5 年内逐步冲减利息收入。同时，不再提取坏账准备金，并停止执行坏账核销的政策。本行已制定计划于 2005 年底以前核销完全部有关的应收未收利息。

一 应收未收利息的复利不计入损益，在表外核算，实际收回时再计入损益。

## 19 支出确认原则

活期存款按季结息，定期存款和居民活期及定期储蓄存款根据存款金额及存单利率或有效法定存款利率按季计提应付利息。

除在相关的主要会计政策特别说明外，其他支出按权责发生制原则确认。

## 20 买入返售和卖出回购

买入返售和卖出回购按实际收付的款项计价，并按权责发生制确认收入和支出。

买入返售和卖出回购的交易标的包括贷款、债券及贴现票据。

## 21 所得税

本行根据财务报表的税前利润，调整毋须课税或不获减免所得税的收入及支出项目后，计算应纳税利润及企业所得税。按国家税务总局国税函[1996]364号的规定，本行适用33%的所得税税率。

本行将源于对原投行资产计提的呆账准备而产生的收购原投行净资产的账面价值与公允价值的差异确认为商誉。根据财政部、国家税务总局颁布的财税[2002]198号文件《关于中国光大银行核销和处置不良资产有关财务税收问题的通知》的相关规定（附注五、45），有关的呆账准备余额将于未来年度本行核销此等呆账时作为申报所得税的纳税所得额之可抵扣项，因而是重大时间性差异。本行对该可扣税的时间性差异采用纳税影响会计法，对其他所得税会计处理采用应付税款法。

## 22 营业税金及附加

营业税金及附加支出乃由本行各分行向其所在地的税务局缴交。营业税乃按营业收入减金融机构往来利息收入（包括购买金融机构发行的债券取得的利息收入）及国债利息收入后，依据财政部及国家税务总局财税[2001]21号文《关于降低金融保险业营业税税率的通知》，自2003年1月1日起，按5%计提（2002年：6%）。

## 23 金融衍生工具

本行进行外汇远期合同、外汇掉期合同和利率掉期的交易。这些交易主要是为降低代客外汇理财业务给本行所带来的市场风险。本行进行衍生金融工具交易的未交割金额在表外核算。外汇远期合同、外汇掉期合同于资产负债表日的未实现汇兑损益计入当期损益。利率掉期交易的相关损益在合约到期交割时在利润表中确认。

## 24 会计政策变更及影响

当根据法律或会计准则制定机构的要求变更会计政策，或会计政策的变更将更能恰当地反映本行财务报表中的事项或交易时，会计政策才会作出变更。

变更会计政策时采用追溯调整法，除非产生的与以前年度相关的调整金额无法合理地确定，则应采用未来适用法。

## 25 员工退休福利和保障

本行按规定参加由政府机构设立的社会保障体系，包括养老及医疗保险、住房公积金及其他社会保障制度。根据有关规定，保险费及公积金按工资总额的一定比例提取并向劳动和社会保障机构缴纳，相应支出计入当期营业费用。

## 26 关联公司

关联公司是指有能力直接或间接地控制本行或对本行的财务和经营决策施加重大影响的公司。关联公司亦包括处在共同控制或影响下的公司。

## 27 经营租赁

当资产所有权的大部分风险及报酬均由出租人承担或享有时，有关的租赁列为经营租赁。经营租赁支出按租约年期以直线方法计入利润表内。

(b) 本行于 1999 年 3 月 18 日接收原投行时，原投行承办的世界银行转贷款（“世行转贷款”）本息余额美元 8.52 亿元（折合人民币 70.56 亿元）和世行转贷款借款（“世行借款”）本金余额美元 5.86 亿元（折合人民币 48.52 亿元）亦包含于接收的资产和负债中。世行转贷款项目贷款本息与世行借款本金的差额为美元 2.66 亿元（折合人民币 22.04 亿元）。造成差额的原因是原投行为如期归还世行借款，采用部分自有资金垫付还款。

财政部于2000年8月18日颁发财金[2000]61号文件《关于给予中国光大（集团）总公司若干财政政策支持的复函》，允许本行将原投行承办的世行转贷款8.53亿美元放在表外核算，并要求本行对回收的世行转贷款设收款专户，将所回收的世行转贷款专项上缴中央财政。

根据上述文件的规定，于2000年，本行将世行转贷款70.56亿元，及世行借款48.52亿元放在表外核算，而差额22.04亿元作为应收世行转贷款剥离差额记录于“其他应收款”。在未来收回世行转贷款时，先计入其他应付款，报财政部批准后，可按一定比例冲减该差额。

## 20 固定资产清理

固定资产清理余额主要为住房周转金。根据财政部财金[2001]28号文《财政部关于印发<关于金融企业住房制度改革若干财务问题的规定>的通知》的规定，于2001年1月1日起取消住房周转金管理制度。企业应对住房周转金账户进行清理。住房周转金账户清理后的余额如为负数，全部转到固定资产清理账户下单独反映。企业在报经主管财政机关审核后，数额较小的，可一次性计入营业外支出；数额较大的，按不超过5年的期限分年度摊销。

本年度计入营业外支出的住房周转金摊销金额为1.03亿元（2002年：1.03亿元）。

商誉形成的具体过程如下：

于1999年3月18日接收原投行时，原投行的账面净资产为27.59亿元，于2000年度，由于确认原投行外汇营运资本金汇率折算收益7.17亿元，使接收原投行时的账面净资产增加至34.76亿元。截止2000年12月31日，根据财会字[1997]30号《关于印发<企业兼并有关会计处理问题暂行规定>的通知》，此金额记录于资本公积。由于原投行资产质量不佳，原投行的账面净资产与按公允价值确认的净资产间存有差异。于2001年，根据财政部财金函[2001]20号《财政部关于中国光大银行接收原中国投资银行后有关商誉确认入账问题的意见》，允许本行对接收的原投行净资产进行重新核定，并将收购成本与净资产公允价值之间的差额作为商誉处理。对于本行而言，收购成本是零，商誉即为净资产的公允价值考虑递延税项的影响后与收购成本的差额。具体计算过程如下：


<table border=1 style='margin: auto; word-wrap: break-word;'><tr><td style='text-align: center; word-wrap: break-word;'>原投行账面的净资产</td><td style='text-align: center; word-wrap: break-word;'>2,759,425</td></tr><tr><td style='text-align: center; word-wrap: break-word;'>确认外汇营运资金折算差额</td><td style='text-align: center; word-wrap: break-word;'>717,072</td></tr><tr><td style='text-align: center; word-wrap: break-word;'>小计</td><td style='text-align: center; word-wrap: break-word;'>3,476,497</td></tr><tr><td style='text-align: center; word-wrap: break-word;'>按公允价值对资产计提呆账准备</td><td style='text-align: center; word-wrap: break-word;'>(14,779,172)</td></tr><tr><td style='text-align: center; word-wrap: break-word;'>因暂时性差异产生的递延税款借项</td><td style='text-align: center; word-wrap: break-word;'>4,765,940</td></tr><tr><td style='text-align: center; word-wrap: break-word;'>调整后的净负债（即商誉）</td><td style='text-align: center; word-wrap: break-word;'>(6,536,735)</td></tr></table>

根据财政部2001年出具的财金函[2001]20号《财政部关于中国光大银行接收原中国投资银行后有关商誉确认入帐问题的意见》的规定，本行将商誉自接收日起在20年内进行摊销，摊销金额的账务处理已采用追溯调整法。

本行将源于对原投行资产计提的呆账准备而产生的收购原投行净资产的账面价值与公允价值的差异确认为商誉。根据财政部、国家税务总局颁布的财税[2002]198号文件《关于中国光大银行核销和处置不良资产有关财务税收问题的通知》的相关规定（附注五、45），有关的呆账准备余额将于未来年度本行核销此等呆账时作为申报所得税的纳税所得额之可抵扣项，因而是重大时间性差异，确认为递延税资产。

#### 资本公积


<table border=1 style='margin: auto; word-wrap: break-word;'><tr><td style='text-align: center; word-wrap: break-word;'></td><td colspan="2">2003年度</td></tr><tr><td style='text-align: center; word-wrap: break-word;'>年初余额</td><td style='text-align: center; word-wrap: break-word;'>2,293,682</td><td style='text-align: center; word-wrap: break-word;'></td></tr><tr><td style='text-align: center; word-wrap: break-word;'>加：豁免应付国家开发银行利息</td><td style='text-align: center; word-wrap: break-word;'>417,621</td><td style='text-align: center; word-wrap: break-word;'></td></tr><tr><td style='text-align: center; word-wrap: break-word;'>加：资本折算差额</td><td style='text-align: center; word-wrap: break-word;'>1,973</td><td style='text-align: center; word-wrap: break-word;'></td></tr><tr><td style='text-align: center; word-wrap: break-word;'>年末余额</td><td style='text-align: center; word-wrap: break-word;'>2,713,276</td><td style='text-align: center; word-wrap: break-word;'></td></tr></table>

根据财政部2003年签发的财金[2002]186号文《财政部关于国家开发银行拆借中国光大银行人民币50亿元资金有关问题的批复》的规定，同意国家开发银行免除中国光大银行因拆借国家开发银行人民币50亿元资金而形成的应付利息。故本行于2003年度将帐面记录的人民币4.18亿元应付国家开发银行拆借息确认为资本公积。

根据国家税务总局2003年7月30日出具的《关于对中国光大银行向国家开发银行拆借免息有关税务问题的答复函》之回复，本行为接收原投行而向国家开发银行拆借资金的免息可不纳入应纳税所得额，故本行确认的上述资本公积无需缴纳企业所得税。


<table border=1 style='margin: auto; word-wrap: break-word;'><tr><td style='text-align: center; word-wrap: break-word;'></td><td style='text-align: center; word-wrap: break-word;'>法定盈余公积金</td><td style='text-align: center; word-wrap: break-word;'>法定公益金</td><td style='text-align: center; word-wrap: break-word;'>任意盈余公积金</td><td style='text-align: center; word-wrap: break-word;'>合计</td></tr><tr><td style='text-align: center; word-wrap: break-word;'>2002年12月31日</td><td style='text-align: center; word-wrap: break-word;'>386,207</td><td style='text-align: center; word-wrap: break-word;'>372,632</td><td style='text-align: center; word-wrap: break-word;'>1,092,468</td><td style='text-align: center; word-wrap: break-word;'>1,851,307</td></tr><tr><td style='text-align: center; word-wrap: break-word;'>本年提取</td><td style='text-align: center; word-wrap: break-word;'>43,340</td><td style='text-align: center; word-wrap: break-word;'>21,670</td><td style='text-align: center; word-wrap: break-word;'>-</td><td style='text-align: center; word-wrap: break-word;'>65,010</td></tr><tr><td style='text-align: center; word-wrap: break-word;'>2003年12月31日</td><td style='text-align: center; word-wrap: break-word;'>429,547</td><td style='text-align: center; word-wrap: break-word;'>394,302</td><td style='text-align: center; word-wrap: break-word;'>1,092,468</td><td style='text-align: center; word-wrap: break-word;'>1,916,317</td></tr></table>

根据《中华人民共和国公司法》，本行章程及董事会的决议，本行按年度净利润（弥补以前年度亏损后）的10%提取法定盈余公积金，当法定盈余公积金累计额达到股本的50%以上时，可不再提取。法定盈余公积金经有关部门批准后可用于弥补亏损，或者增加股本。除了用于弥补亏损外，法定盈余公积金于增加股本后，其余额不得少于股本的25%。根据本行董事会于2004年4月9日的决议，本行2003年度按净利润的10%提取法定盈余公积金4,334万元（2002年：2,715.8万元）。

另外按年度净利润（弥补以前年度亏损后）的5%至10%提取法定公益金，用于员工的集体福利而不用于股东分配。根据本行董事会于2004年4月9日的决议，本行2003年度按净利润的5%提取法定公益金2,167万元（2002年：5%，等同1,357.9万元）。

本行任意盈余公积金的提取额由董事会提议，并经股东大会批准。在得到相应的批准后，任意盈余公积金方可用于弥补以前年度亏损或增加股本。根据本行董事会于2004年4月9日的决议，本行2003年不提取任意盈余公积金（2002年：未提取）。

word-wrap: break-word;'>449,845</td></tr></table>

根据《中华人民共和国公司法》及本行章程，于每年利润分配时，本行须根据中国会计准则确定的净利润按下列顺序分配：

(a) 弥补以前年度亏损；

(b) 提取 10% 的法定公积金和提取 5% – 10% 的法定公益金；

(c) 经股东大会决议提取任意盈余公积金；

(d) 支付股利。

本行亦根据财政部颁布的财金[2001]127号文《财政部关于印发〈金融企业呆账准备提取及呆账核销管理办法〉的通知》的规定，呆帐准备需在2005年底以前根据资产的风险程度足额提取，除得到相关监管部门的

批准外，呆帐准备提取不足的，不得进行税后利润分配。

根据2003年7月11日股东大会的批准，本行以股份总额82.1689亿为基数，每股派发2002年度股利0.04元，共计派发股利3.28676亿元。该股利分配方案已上报中国人民银行。

#### 所得税


<table border=1 style='margin: auto; word-wrap: break-word;'><tr><td style='text-align: center; word-wrap: break-word;'></td><td style='text-align: center; word-wrap: break-word;'>2003年度</td><td style='text-align: center; word-wrap: break-word;'>2002年度</td></tr><tr><td style='text-align: center; word-wrap: break-word;'>递延税项转回（附注五、23）</td><td style='text-align: center; word-wrap: break-word;'>381,681</td><td style='text-align: center; word-wrap: break-word;'>340,078</td></tr></table>

根据财政部、国家税务总局颁布的财税[2002]198号文件《关于中国光大银行核销和处置不良资产有关财务税收问题的通知》中，对本行在核销和处置原投行不良资产的有关财务税收政策规定：

一 本行在 2001 年至 2009 年期间，可以用为原投行不良资产提取的 144 亿元专项准备金核销原投行的呆账和处置原投行的不良资产。

－ 在上述期间内，本行接收原投行不良资产形成的损失，凡符合税收规定呆账损失条件的，经本行总行报主管税务部门审核确认后，准予按实际损失额直接在企业所得税前扣除。

实际所得税费用不同于按法定税率计算的税额，主要调节事项如下：


<table border=1 style='margin: auto; word-wrap: break-word;'><tr><td style='text-align: center; word-wrap: break-word;'></td><td style='text-align: center; word-wrap: break-word;'>2003年度</td><td style='text-align: center; word-wrap: break-word;'>2002年度</td></tr><tr><td style='text-align: center; word-wrap: break-word;'>税前利润</td><td style='text-align: center; word-wrap: break-word;'>815,087</td><td style='text-align: center; word-wrap: break-word;'>611,657</td></tr><tr><td style='text-align: center; word-wrap: break-word;'>按照33%的所得税率计提所得税</td><td style='text-align: center; word-wrap: break-word;'>268,978</td><td style='text-align: center; word-wrap: break-word;'>201,847</td></tr><tr><td style='text-align: center; word-wrap: break-word;'>免税国债利息收入的影响</td><td style='text-align: center; word-wrap: break-word;'>(475,028)</td><td style='text-align: center; word-wrap: break-word;'>(331,153)</td></tr><tr><td style='text-align: center; word-wrap: break-word;'>不可税前抵扣的工资福利、业务招待费及其他费用的影响</td><td style='text-align: center; word-wrap: break-word;'>587,731</td><td style='text-align: center; word-wrap: break-word;'>469,384</td></tr><tr><td style='text-align: center; word-wrap: break-word;'>所得税费用</td><td style='text-align: center; word-wrap: break-word;'>381,681</td><td style='text-align: center; word-wrap: break-word;'>340,078</td></tr></table>

于2003年，本行核销呆帐28.66亿元（2002年：23.82亿元），经有关部门批准，当期将应纳所得税金额冲减递延税款借项，冲减金额以应纳税所得额的纳税影响为限。

## 六 关联方余额及交易

股东及光大集团企业的拆出资金、贷款和存款：


<table border=1 style='margin: auto; word-wrap: break-word;'><tr><td style='text-align: center; word-wrap: break-word;'></td><td style='text-align: center; word-wrap: break-word;'>2003年12月31日</td><td style='text-align: center; word-wrap: break-word;'>2002年12月31日</td></tr><tr><td style='text-align: center; word-wrap: break-word;'>对光大集团总公司的贷款（a）</td><td style='text-align: center; word-wrap: break-word;'>2,163,752</td><td style='text-align: center; word-wrap: break-word;'>2,163,773</td></tr><tr><td style='text-align: center; word-wrap: break-word;'>对光大集团下属企业的拆出资金和贷款</td><td style='text-align: center; word-wrap: break-word;'>2,626,430</td><td style='text-align: center; word-wrap: break-word;'>2,972,909</td></tr><tr><td style='text-align: center; word-wrap: break-word;'>对其他股东的贷款和贴现</td><td style='text-align: center; word-wrap: break-word;'>5,382,920</td><td style='text-align: center; word-wrap: break-word;'>4,258,800</td></tr><tr><td style='text-align: center; word-wrap: break-word;'>光大集团总公司的存款</td><td style='text-align: center; word-wrap: break-word;'>131,336</td><td style='text-align: center; word-wrap: break-word;'>88,193</td></tr><tr><td style='text-align: center; word-wrap: break-word;'>光大集团下属企业的存款</td><td style='text-align: center; word-wrap: break-word;'>1,137,946</td><td style='text-align: center; word-wrap: break-word;'>2,562,021</td></tr><tr><td style='text-align: center; word-wrap: break-word;'>其他股东的存款</td><td style='text-align: center; word-wrap: break-word;'>1,703,621</td><td style='text-align: center; word-wrap: break-word;'>2,221,545</td></tr></table>

所有关联公司的贷款及存款的利息收入及支出均按现行商业利率计算。

(a) 截至 2003 年 12 月 31 日止，对光大集团总公司贷款余额为 21.64 亿元，光大集团总公司将持有本行 24.16% 的股权质押给青岛京太置业有限公司，并由后者为上述贷款提供担保。

2 于 2003 年 12 月 31 日，由光大集团作为贷款担保方由本行发放予第三方的贷款余额约为 2,180 万元（2002 年：8,900 万元）。

3 于2003年12月31日，由本行为股东提供的担保余额约为24亿元（2002年：26.05亿元）。其中，包括为光大集团发行的3年期金融债券21亿元（2002年：21亿元）的担保。该债券于1997年6月26日发行，利率为9.18%。截止2003年12月31日，该债券已逾期42个月。光大集团未能履行还本付息责任，共欠本息26.78亿元（2002年：26.78亿元）。根据中国人民银行银发[2002]64－67号《中国人民银行关于处理中国光大（集团）总公司有关债务问题的通知》，同意光大集团延迟还款，具体还款计划将由中国人民银行制定。因此，本行认为于2003年12月31日尽管上述债券已逾期，本行无须对其承担偿付义务。

2003 年度本行与光大集团总公司及其关联公司进行的重大交易有：

于2003年8月31日与光大财务有限公司续签两份外汇拆借资金展期合同美元1.46亿元，港币1.62亿元。于2003年1月光大证券股份有限公司偿还向光大银行的拆借资金9.23亿元；于2003年12月30－31日光大证券股份有限公司向光大银行拆借资金5亿元。

REPORT OF THE AUDITORS

[English Translation for Reference only]

To the Shareholders of China Everbright Bank Co., Ltd.:

We have audited the accompanying balance sheet of China Everbright Bank Co., Ltd. (the "Bank") as at 31 December 2003 and its profit and loss account and cash flow statement for the year then ended. These financial statements set out on pages 120 to 153 are the responsibility of the Bank's management. Our responsibility is to express an audit opinion on the financial statements based on our audit.

We conducted our audit in accordance with China's Independent Auditing Standards. Those Standards require that we plan and perform the audit to obtain reasonable assurance about whether the financial statements are free of material misstatement. An audit includes examining, on a test basis, evidence supporting the amounts and disclosures in the financial statements. An audit also includes assessing the accounting policies used and significant accounting estimates made by management, as well as evaluating the overall financial statement presentation. We believe that our audit provides a reasonable basis for our opinion.

In our opinion, the financial statements referred to above conform with the applicable Accounting Standards for Business Enterprises and the Accounting System for Financial Institutions promulgated by the Ministry of Finance of the People's Republic of China in 1993 and the relevant accounting rules for banks mentioned in the notes to the financial statements, and present fairly, in all material respects, the financial position of the Bank as at 31 December 2003 and the results of its operations and cash flows for the year then ended.

PricewaterhouseCoopers Zhong Tian

CPAs Limited Company
